### CLAUDE.md
    1: # PaymentCalcs â€” Working Rules
    2: 
    3: Canonical spec: `docs/PaymentCalcs_PRD_v2.md` (Â§0.0 Merge Record overrides base text).
    4: Build directive: `docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md`. PRD wins on conflict â€” flag in `PROGRESS.md`.
    5: Session protocol: read this file, `PROGRESS.md`, and the current phase's PRD sections at session start; update `PROGRESS.md` + `VERIFICATION-QUEUE.md` at session end.
    6: 
    7: ## Non-negotiables (release-gating)
    8: 
    9: 1. **No statutory number from memory.** Every jurisdiction/year-dependent value lives in `packages/rules-au/` with source URL, `retrievedAt`, content hash, and status. Packs authored here are always `in_review`, never `active` â€” activation is a human action. If a source can't be fetched, the pack ships with `null` values.
   10: 2. **Engines are pure:** `(request, rulePacks) => result` per Â§14. No network, no `Date.now()`, no UI imports. Money = integer minor units serialised as strings (branded type, never `number`); rate maths uses `decimal.js`.
   11: 3. **E02 (annual liability) â‰  E03 (withholding).** Separate engines, separately labelled outputs. Never annual Ã· periods where an official schedule applies.
   12: 4. **Mortgage = scheduled ledger** (payment-period, daily-capable contracts, daily mode NOT built at P0).
   13: 5. **Reconciliation failure = engine failure**, never a cosmetic warning (Â§13.30).
   14: 6. **Solvers:** monotonic bisection with Â§13.28 safeguards; no plausible numbers after failed convergence.
   15: 7. **No accounts/DB/auth/server storage.** IndexedDB save; versioned URL share; client-side calc; heavy schedules in a Web Worker.
   16: 8. **Analytics only via `packages/analytics-safe`** â€” rejects money/income/balance/rate-as-fact/age/free-text props; tests prove rejection. Plausible; no cookies.
   17: 9. **No LLM in any calculation path.**
   18: 10. **No super/retirement routes at P0.**
   19: 11. **Disclosures** render versioned Â§17.9 copy verbatim. Result surfaces never say "should", "we recommend", "best for you" (lint + content test).
   20: 12. **Fail closed:** unresolvable/integrity-failed rule pack â‡’ "Rule unavailable" state, never a wrong number. `PC_ALLOW_DRAFT_RULES=1` runs `in_review` packs in dev only, behind a persistent DRAFT banner; impossible in production builds.
   21: 
   22: ## Conventions
   23: 
   24: - Stack (locked): Next.js App Router Â· React Â· TS strict Â· Tailwind 4 + `design-tokens` Â· pnpm + Turborepo Â· Zod Â· decimal.js Â· Temporal polyfill Â· Vitest Â· fast-check Â· Playwright + axe Â· Recharts (dynamic). **No new runtime deps without asking.**
   25: - Verify before claiming: run affected suites after every change; full `pnpm verify` before ending a session. Conventional commits; every commit green.
   26: - Golden fixtures ship with `expected: null` for the owner to fill from official calculators; CI counts unverified goldens. Boundary/property/metamorphic/differential tests are self-owned and always on.
   27: - Design: STRATA Swiss-editorial aesthetic (owner-directed, `docs/DESIGN.md`) merged with PRD Â§20 functional rules â€” see PROGRESS.md decision log. Every monetary value renders in mono with `tabular-nums`.

   31: 
   32: /**
   33:  * Modelling convention, not a statutory value: GST amounts round half-up to
   34:  * the cent. Surfaced as a contract-setting assumption and in the Working tab.
   35:  */
   36: export const AU_GST_ROUNDING: RoundingProfile = {
   37:   id: "au-gst-half-up-cents",
   38:   intermediateScale: 6,
   39:   moneyDisplayScale: 2,
   40:   mode: "half_up",
   41:   roundEachPeriod: false,
   42:   finalPaymentAdjustment: false,
   43:   reconciliationToleranceMinorUnits: 0,
   44: };

   46: export interface GstEngineContext {
   47:   /** Injected clock â€” engines never read the system time (Â§11.2 determinism). */
   48:   now: ISODateTime;
   49: }
   50: 
   51: export interface GstResolution {
   52:   pack: GstRulePack;
   53:   manifestRef: RulePackManifestRef;
   54: }
   55: 
   56: interface EngineOutcome {
   57:   status: CalculationResultV1<GstOutput>["status"];
   58:   output?: GstOutput;
   59:   warnings: CalculationMessage[];
   60:   errors: CalculationMessage[];
   61:   assumptions: AssumptionRecord[];

   63:   trace?: { level: "summary" | "full"; steps: TraceStep[] };
   64:   reconciliation?: ReconciliationV1[];
   65: }
   66: 
   67: export async function calculateGst(
   68:   request: CalculationRequestV1<GstInput>,
   69:   resolution: GstResolution,
   70:   context: GstEngineContext,
   71: ): Promise<CalculationResultV1<GstOutput>> {
   72:   const canonicalRequestHash = await canonicalHash(request);
   73:   const outcome = computeGst(request, resolution);
   74:   const withoutIntegrity = {
   75:     requestId: request.requestId,
   76:     calculationId: `calc_${canonicalRequestHash.slice(0, 16)}`,
   77:     calculatorId: request.calculatorId,
   78:     calculationClass: "A" as const,
   79:     calculatedAt: context.now,
   80:     engineVersions: { [GST_ENGINE_ID]: GST_ENGINE_VERSION },
   81:     rulePacks: [resolution.manifestRef],
   82:     ...outcome,
   83:   };
   84:   return {
   85:     ...withoutIntegrity,
   86:     integrity: {
   87:       canonicalRequestHash,
   88:       canonicalResultHash: await canonicalHash(withoutIntegrity),
   89:     },
   90:   };
   91: }
   92: 
   93: /** Pure synchronous core â€” everything except hashing. */
   94: export function computeGst(
   95:   request: CalculationRequestV1<GstInput>,
   96:   resolution: GstResolution,
   97: ): EngineOutcome {
   98:   const parsed = zGstInput.safeParse(request.input);
   99:   if (!parsed.success) {
  100:     return {
  101:       status: "invalid",
  102:       warnings: [],
  103:       errors: parsed.error.issues.map(
  104:         (issue): CalculationMessage => ({
  105:           code: "PC-VAL-0002",
  106:           severity: "error",
  107:           message: issue.message,
  108:           path: issue.path.map(String),
  109:         }),
  110:       ),
  111:       assumptions: [],
  112:       sources: [],
  113:     };
  114:   }
  115:   const input = parsed.data;
  116: 
  117:   const rateString = resolution.pack.rules.standardRate;
  118:   if (rateString === null) {
  119:     return {
  120:       status: "failed",
  121:       warnings: [],
  122:       errors: [
  123:         {
  124:           code: "PC-RULE-0004",
  125:           severity: "error",
  126:           message:
  127:             "The GST rate in the resolved rule pack has not been populated from an official source.",
  128:         },
  129:       ],
  130:       assumptions: [],
  131:       sources: [],
  132:     };
  133:   }
  134: 
  135:   const rate = dec(rateString);
  136:   const onePlusRate = new Dec(1).plus(rate) as DecimalValue;
  137:   const mode = AU_GST_ROUNDING.mode;
  138:   const warnings: CalculationMessage[] = [];
  139:   const trace: TraceStep[] = [];
  140:   let output: GstOutput;
  141: 
  142:   if (input.mode === "add") {
  143:     const exclusive = input.amount;
  144:     const gst = multiplyMoney(exclusive, rate, mode);
  145:     const inclusive = addMoney(exclusive, gst);
  146:     trace.push(
  147:       {
  148:         id: "step-1",
  149:         formulaId: "F-GST-001",
  150:         label: "GST on the exclusive amount",
  151:         expression: "gst = exclusive Ã— g",
  152:         substitution: `gst = ${moneyToDecimalString(exclusive)} Ã— ${rateString}`,
  153:         value: moneyToDecimalString(gst),
  154:         unit: exclusive.currency,
  155:       },
  156:       {
  157:         id: "step-2",
  158:         formulaId: "F-GST-001",
  159:         label: "GST-inclusive total",
  160:         expression: "inclusive = exclusive + gst",
  161:         substitution: `inclusive = ${moneyToDecimalString(exclusive)} + ${moneyToDecimalString(gst)}`,
  162:         value: moneyToDecimalString(inclusive),
  163:         unit: exclusive.currency,
  164:       },
  165:     );
  166:     output = {
  167:       mode: input.mode,
  168:       rate: rateString,
  169:       exclusiveAmount: exclusive,
  170:       gstAmount: gst,
  171:       inclusiveAmount: inclusive,
  172:     };
  173:   } else if (input.mode === "remove" || input.mode === "split") {
  174:     const inclusive = input.amount;
  175:     const exactGstMinor = dec(inclusive.minorUnits).times(rate).div(onePlusRate) as DecimalValue;
  176:     const gst = moneyFromDecimalMinorUnits(inclusive.currency, inclusive.scale, exactGstMinor, mode);
  177:     const exclusive = subtractMoney(inclusive, gst);
  178:     trace.push(
  179:       {
  180:         id: "step-1",
  181:         formulaId: "F-GST-002",
  182:         label: "GST component of the inclusive amount",
  183:         expression: "gst = inclusive Ã— g Ã· (1 + g)",
  184:         substitution: `gst = ${moneyToDecimalString(inclusive)} Ã— ${rateString} Ã· ${onePlusRate.toString()}`,
  185:         value: moneyToDecimalString(gst),
  186:         unit: inclusive.currency,
  187:       },
  188:       {
  189:         id: "step-2",
  190:         formulaId: "F-GST-002",
  191:         label: "GST-exclusive amount",
  192:         expression: "exclusive = inclusive âˆ’ gst",
  193:         substitution: `exclusive = ${moneyToDecimalString(inclusive)} âˆ’ ${moneyToDecimalString(gst)}`,
  194:         value: moneyToDecimalString(exclusive),
  195:         unit: inclusive.currency,
  196:       },
  197:     );
  198:     output = {
  199:       mode: input.mode,
  200:       rate: rateString,
  201:       exclusiveAmount: exclusive,
  202:       gstAmount: gst,
  203:       inclusiveAmount: inclusive,
  204:     };
  205:   } else {
  206:     const first = input.items[0]!.amount;
  207:     const currency = first.currency;
  208:     const scale = first.scale;
  209:     for (const item of input.items) {
  210:       if (item.amount.currency !== currency || item.amount.scale !== scale) {
  211:         return {
  212:           status: "invalid",
  213:           warnings: [],
  214:           errors: [
  215:             {
  216:               code: "PC-VAL-0003",
  217:               severity: "error",
  218:               message: "All line items must use the same currency.",

  221:           ],
  222:           assumptions: [],
  223:           sources: [],
  224:         };
  225:       }
  226:     }
  227: 
  228:     const lines: GstLineResult[] = [];
  229:     let exactGstTotalMinor = new Dec(0) as DecimalValue;
  230:     let lastTaxableIndex = -1;
  231: 
  232:     for (const item of input.items) {
  233:       const lineAmount = multiplyMoney(item.amount, new Dec(item.quantity) as DecimalValue, mode);
  234:       let exclusive: Money;
  235:       let gst: Money;
  236:       let inclusive: Money;
  237: 
  238:       if (item.treatment !== "taxable") {
  239:         exclusive = lineAmount;
  240:         gst = zeroMoney(currency, scale);
  241:         inclusive = lineAmount;
  242:       } else if (item.amountIs === "exclusive") {
  243:         exclusive = lineAmount;
  244:         const exactGstMinor = dec(exclusive.minorUnits).times(rate) as DecimalValue;
  245:         gst = moneyFromDecimalMinorUnits(currency, scale, exactGstMinor, mode);
  246:         inclusive = addMoney(exclusive, gst);
  247:         exactGstTotalMinor = exactGstTotalMinor.plus(exactGstMinor) as DecimalValue;
  248:         lastTaxableIndex = lines.length;
  249:       } else {
  250:         inclusive = lineAmount;
  251:         const exactGstMinor = dec(inclusive.minorUnits)
  252:           .times(rate)
  253:           .div(onePlusRate) as DecimalValue;
  254:         gst = moneyFromDecimalMinorUnits(currency, scale, exactGstMinor, mode);
  255:         exclusive = subtractMoney(inclusive, gst);
  256:         exactGstTotalMinor = exactGstTotalMinor.plus(exactGstMinor) as DecimalValue;
  257:         lastTaxableIndex = lines.length;
  258:       }
  259: 
  260:       lines.push({
  261:         id: item.id,
  262:         ...(item.label !== undefined ? { label: item.label } : {}),
  263:         treatment: item.treatment,
  264:         quantity: item.quantity,
  265:         exclusiveAmount: exclusive,
  266:         gstAmount: gst,
  267:         inclusiveAmount: inclusive,
  268:       });
  269:     }
  270: 
  271:     if (input.roundingLevel === "invoice_total" && lastTaxableIndex >= 0) {
  272:       // Authoritative GST is the exact total rounded once; any difference from
  273:       // the per-line rounding is assigned to the last taxable line so lines

  275:       const authoritativeGst = moneyFromDecimalMinorUnits(currency, scale, exactGstTotalMinor, mode);
  276:       const summedLineGst = sumMoney(currency, scale, lines.map((l) => l.gstAmount));
  277:       const adjustment = subtractMoney(authoritativeGst, summedLineGst);
  278:       if (adjustment.minorUnits !== "0") {

  282:         warnings.push({

  286:         });
  287:       }
  288:     }
  289: 
  290:     const exclusiveTotal = sumMoney(currency, scale, lines.map((l) => l.exclusiveAmount));
  291:     const gstTotal = sumMoney(currency, scale, lines.map((l) => l.gstAmount));
  292:     const inclusiveTotal = sumMoney(currency, scale, lines.map((l) => l.inclusiveAmount));
  293: 
  294:     trace.push({
  295:       id: "step-1",
  296:       formulaId: "F-GST-001",
  297:       label: `Line GST, ${input.roundingLevel === "per_line" ? "rounded per line" : "rounded at the invoice total"}`,

  304:     output = {

  311:       lines,
  312:     };
  313:   }
  314: 
  315:   const zero = zeroMoney(output.exclusiveAmount.currency, output.exclusiveAmount.scale);
  316:   if (compareMoney(output.exclusiveAmount, zero) < 0) {

  324:   const closing = addMoney(output.exclusiveAmount, output.gstAmount);

  329:     closingAmount: closing,
  330:     expectedClosingAmount: output.inclusiveAmount,
  331:     difference: subtractMoney(closing, output.inclusiveAmount),
  332:     tolerance: zero,
  333:     passed: compareMoney(closing, output.inclusiveAmount) === 0,
  334:   };
  335: 
  336:   if (!reconciliation.passed) {
  337:     return {
  338:       status: "failed",
  339:       warnings,
  340:       errors: [
  341:         {
  342:           code: "PC-REC-0001",
  343:           severity: "error",
  344:           message: "GST reconciliation failed: exclusive + GST does not equal inclusive.",
  345:         },
  346:       ],
  347:       assumptions: [],
  348:       sources: [],
  349:       reconciliation: [reconciliation],
  350:     };
  351:   }
  352: 
  353:   const gstSource = resolution.pack.sources[0];
  354:   const sources: SourceRef[] = gstSource
  355:     ? [
  356:         {
  357:           sourceId: gstSource.sourceId,
  358:           title: gstSource.title,
  359:           url: gstSource.url,
  360:           authority: gstSource.authority,
  361:           retrievedAt: gstSource.retrievedAt,
  362:           rulePackId: resolution.pack.rulePackId,
  363:         },
  364:       ]
  365:     : [];
  366: 
  367:   const assumptions: AssumptionRecord[] = [
  368:     {
  369:       id: "gst-standard-rate",
  370:       label: "Standard GST rate",
  371:       category: "official_rule",
  372:       value: rateString,
  373:       unit: "rate",
  374:       editable: false,
  375:       materiality: "high",
  376:       ...(gstSource ? { sourceId: gstSource.sourceId } : {}),
  377:       explanation: `Standard GST rate from rule pack ${resolution.pack.rulePackId} (${resolution.pack.rulesVersion}).`,
  378:     },
  379:     {
  380:       id: "gst-rounding",
  381:       label: "Rounding",
  382:       category: "contract_setting",
  383:       value:
  384:         input.mode === "line_items"
  385:           ? `half-up to the cent, ${input.roundingLevel === "per_line" ? "per line" : "at invoice total"}`
  386:           : "half-up to the cent",
  387:       editable: input.mode === "line_items",
  388:       materiality: "low",
  389:       explanation:
  390:         "GST amounts are rounded half-up to the nearest cent. On invoices, rounding can apply per line or once at the invoice total.",
  391:     },
  392:     {
  393:       id: "gst-treatment",
  394:       label: "GST treatment",
  395:       category: "user_input",
  396:       value: input.mode === "line_items" ? "per line item" : "taxable supply",
  397:       editable: true,
  398:       materiality: "high",
  399:       explanation:
  400:         "Whether a supply is taxable, GST-free or input taxed is your selection. It is never inferred from item names, and this tool does not determine registration or entitlement to input tax credits.",
  401:     },
  402:   ];
  403: 
  404:   return {
  405:     status: warnings.some((w) => w.severity === "warning") ? "success_with_warnings" : "success",
  406:     output,
  407:     warnings,
  408:     errors: [],
  409:     assumptions,
  410:     sources,
  411:     ...(request.options.traceLevel !== "none" ? { trace: { level: "full" as const, steps: trace } } : {}),
  412:     reconciliation: [reconciliation],
  413:   };
  414: }
### packages/engine-business/src/gst/schema.ts
    1: import { z } from "zod";
    2: import { zMoney } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    3: import type { Money } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    4: 
    5: /**
    6:  * AU-BIZ-001 input, schema version 1 (Â§12.15).
    7:  * GST treatment is always explicit â€” never inferred from item labels.
    8:  */
    9: export const GST_TREATMENTS = ["taxable", "gst_free", "input_taxed"] as const;
   10: export type GstTreatment = (typeof GST_TREATMENTS)[number];
   11: 
   12: export const zGstLineItem = z.object({
   13:   id: z.string().min(1),
   14:   label: z.string().max(120).optional(),
   15:   amount: zMoney,
   16:   amountIs: z.enum(["exclusive", "inclusive"]),
   17:   treatment: z.enum(GST_TREATMENTS),
   18:   quantity: z.number().int().min(1).max(9999).default(1),
   19: });
   20: 
   21: export const zGstInput = z.discriminatedUnion("mode", [
   22:   z.object({ mode: z.literal("add"), amount: zMoney }),
   23:   z.object({ mode: z.literal("remove"), amount: zMoney }),
   24:   z.object({ mode: z.literal("split"), amount: zMoney }),
   25:   z.object({
   26:     mode: z.literal("line_items"),
   27:     items: z.array(zGstLineItem).min(1).max(200),
   28:     roundingLevel: z.enum(["per_line", "invoice_total"]),
   29:   }),
   30: ]);
   31: 
   32: export type GstInput = z.infer<typeof zGstInput>;
   33: export type GstLineItem = z.infer<typeof zGstLineItem>;
   34: 
   35: export interface GstLineResult {
   36:   id: string;
   37:   label?: string;
   38:   treatment: GstTreatment;
   39:   quantity: number;
   40:   exclusiveAmount: Money;
   41:   gstAmount: Money;
   42:   inclusiveAmount: Money;
   43: }
   44: 
   45: export interface GstOutput {
   46:   mode: GstInput["mode"];
   47:   /** Dimensionless decimal string from the rule pack, e.g. "0.10". */
   48:   rate: string;
   49:   exclusiveAmount: Money;
   50:   gstAmount: Money;
   51:   inclusiveAmount: Money;
   52:   roundingLevel?: "per_line" | "invoice_total";
   53:   lines?: GstLineResult[];
   54: }
### packages/engine-business/src/gst/engine.test.ts
    1: import fc from "fast-check";
    2: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
    3: import {
    4:   addMoney,
    5:   compareMoney,
    6:   moneyFromMinorUnits,
    7:   type CalculationRequestV1,
    8: } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    9: import { gstPack } from "@paymentcalcs/rules-au";
   10: import { calculateGst, computeGst, type GstResolution } from "./engine";
   11: import type { GstInput } from "./schema";
   12: 
   13: const aud = (minor: string) => moneyFromMinorUnits("AUD", minor, 2);
   14: 
   15: const resolution: GstResolution = {
   16:   pack: gstPack,
   17:   manifestRef: {
   18:     rulePackId: gstPack.rulePackId,
   19:     rulesVersion: gstPack.rulesVersion,
   20:     status: gstPack.status,
   21:     integritySha256: "0".repeat(64),
   22:   },
   23: };
   24: 
   25: function makeRequest(input: GstInput): CalculationRequestV1<GstInput> {
   26:   return {
   27:     requestId: "req-test",
   28:     calculatorId: "AU-BIZ-001",
   29:     calculatorSchemaVersion: "1",
   30:     jurisdiction: { country: "AU" },
   31:     locale: "en-AU",
   32:     currency: "AUD",
   33:     valuationDate: "2026-08-20",
   34:     input,
   35:     options: { traceLevel: "full" },
   36:   };
   37: }
   38: 
   39: function run(input: GstInput) {
   40:   return computeGst(makeRequest(input), resolution);
   41: }
   42: 
   43: describe("GST boundaries (Â§12.15 acceptance criteria)", () => {
   44:   it("adds GST to $100.00 exactly", () => {
   45:     const r = run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("10000") });
   46:     expect(r.status).toBe("success");
   47:     expect(r.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("1000");
   48:     expect(r.output?.inclusiveAmount.minorUnits).toBe("11000");
   49:   });
   50: 
   51:   it("handles zero and one-cent amounts without error", () => {
   52:     expect(run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("0") }).output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("0");
   53:     const oneCent = run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("1") });
   54:     // 10% of 1c = 0.1c â†’ rounds half-up to 0c.
   55:     expect(oneCent.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("0");
   56:     expect(run({ mode: "split", amount: aud("1") }).output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("0");
   57:   });
   58: 
   59:   it("rounds the half-cent boundary half-up: 10% of $0.05 = 1c", () => {
   60:     const r = run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("5") });
   61:     expect(r.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("1");
   62:   });
   63: 
   64:   it("splits a GST-inclusive $110.00 into $100.00 + $10.00", () => {
   65:     const r = run({ mode: "split", amount: aud("11000") });
   66:     expect(r.output?.exclusiveAmount.minorUnits).toBe("10000");
   67:     expect(r.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("1000");
   68:   });
   69: 
   70:   it("flags negative amounts as credits with a warning, still calculating", () => {
   71:     const r = run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("-10000") });
   72:     expect(r.status).toBe("success_with_warnings");
   73:     expect(r.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("-1000");
   74:   });
   75: 
   76:   it("never infers treatment from labels: a line labelled 'milk' is taxed when marked taxable", () => {
   77:     const r = run({
   78:       mode: "line_items",
   79:       roundingLevel: "per_line",
   80:       items: [
   81:         { id: "l1", label: "milk (GST-free in real life)", amount: aud("1000"), amountIs: "exclusive", treatment: "taxable", quantity: 1 },
   82:         { id: "l2", label: "consulting", amount: aud("1000"), amountIs: "exclusive", treatment: "gst_free", quantity: 1 },
   83:       ],
   84:     });
   85:     expect(r.output?.lines?.[0]?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("100");
   86:     expect(r.output?.lines?.[1]?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("0");
   87:   });
   88: 
   89:   it("fails closed when the pack rate is null", () => {
   90:     const nullPack = { ...gstPack, rules: { standardRate: null } };
   91:     const r = computeGst(makeRequest({ mode: "add", amount: aud("100") }), {
   92:       ...resolution,
   93:       pack: nullPack,
   94:     });
   95:     expect(r.status).toBe("failed");
   96:     expect(r.errors[0]?.code).toBe("PC-RULE-0004");
   97:     expect(r.output).toBeUndefined();
   98:   });
   99: 
  100:   it("rejects malformed input as invalid, not thrown", () => {
  101:     const r = computeGst(
  102:       makeRequest({ mode: "add", amount: { bad: true } } as unknown as GstInput),
  103:       resolution,
  104:     );
  105:     expect(r.status).toBe("invalid");
  106:     expect(r.errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  107:   });
  108: });
  109: 
  110: const minorUnitsArb = fc
  111:   .bigInt({ min: -99999999999n, max: 99999999999n })
  112:   .map((n) => aud(n.toString()));
  113: 
  114: describe("GST properties", () => {
  115:   it("reconciliation always passes and is exact: exclusive + gst = inclusive", () => {
  116:     fc.assert(
  117:       fc.property(minorUnitsArb, fc.constantFrom<GstInput["mode"]>("add", "remove", "split"), (amount, mode) => {
  118:         const r = run({ mode, amount } as GstInput);
  119:         expect(r.reconciliation?.[0]?.passed).toBe(true);
  120:         const out = r.output!;
  121:         expect(addMoney(out.exclusiveAmount, out.gstAmount).minorUnits).toBe(
  122:           out.inclusiveAmount.minorUnits,
  123:         );
  124:       }),
  125:     );
  126:   });
  127: 
  128:   it("GST is monotone non-decreasing in the amount", () => {
  129:     fc.assert(
  130:       fc.property(minorUnitsArb, minorUnitsArb, (a, b) => {
  131:         const [lo, hi] = compareMoney(a, b) <= 0 ? [a, b] : [b, a];
  132:         const gstLo = run({ mode: "add", amount: lo }).output!.gstAmount;
  133:         const gstHi = run({ mode: "add", amount: hi }).output!.gstAmount;
  134:         expect(compareMoney(gstLo, gstHi)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
  135:       }),
  136:     );
  137:   });
  138: 
  139:   it("metamorphic: add then split round-trips within one cent", () => {
  140:     fc.assert(
  141:       fc.property(minorUnitsArb, (exclusive) => {
  142:         const added = run({ mode: "add", amount: exclusive }).output!;
  143:         const split = run({ mode: "split", amount: added.inclusiveAmount }).output!;
  144:         const diff = BigInt(split.exclusiveAmount.minorUnits) - BigInt(exclusive.minorUnits);
  145:         expect(diff <= 1n && diff >= -1n).toBe(true);
  146:       }),
  147:     );
  148:   });
  149: 
  150:   it("line items: lines always sum exactly to totals at both rounding levels", () => {
  151:     const itemArb = fc.record({
  152:       id: fc.uuid(),
  153:       amount: fc.bigInt({ min: 1n, max: 9999999n }).map((n) => aud(n.toString())),
  154:       amountIs: fc.constantFrom("exclusive" as const, "inclusive" as const),
  155:       treatment: fc.constantFrom("taxable" as const, "gst_free" as const, "input_taxed" as const),
  156:       quantity: fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 20 }),
  157:     });
  158:     fc.assert(
  159:       fc.property(
  160:         fc.array(itemArb, { minLength: 1, maxLength: 12 }),
  161:         fc.constantFrom("per_line" as const, "invoice_total" as const),
  162:         (items, roundingLevel) => {
  163:           const r = run({ mode: "line_items", items, roundingLevel });
  164:           expect(r.status === "success" || r.status === "success_with_warnings").toBe(true);
  165:           const out = r.output!;
  166:           const sum = (key: "exclusiveAmount" | "gstAmount" | "inclusiveAmount") =>
  167:             out.lines!.reduce((acc, l) => acc + BigInt(l[key].minorUnits), 0n).toString();
  168:           expect(sum("exclusiveAmount")).toBe(out.exclusiveAmount.minorUnits);
  169:           expect(sum("gstAmount")).toBe(out.gstAmount.minorUnits);
  170:           expect(sum("inclusiveAmount")).toBe(out.inclusiveAmount.minorUnits);
  171:         },
  172:       ),
  173:     );
  174:   });
  175: 
  176:   it("per-line vs invoice-total totals differ by at most half a cent per taxable line", () => {
  177:     const itemArb = fc.record({
  178:       id: fc.uuid(),
  179:       amount: fc.bigInt({ min: 1n, max: 999999n }).map((n) => aud(n.toString())),
  180:       amountIs: fc.constantFrom("exclusive" as const, "inclusive" as const),
  181:       treatment: fc.constant("taxable" as const),
  182:       quantity: fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 5 }),
  183:     });
  184:     fc.assert(
  185:       fc.property(fc.array(itemArb, { minLength: 1, maxLength: 10 }), (items) => {
  186:         const perLine = run({ mode: "line_items", items, roundingLevel: "per_line" }).output!;
  187:         const invoice = run({ mode: "line_items", items, roundingLevel: "invoice_total" }).output!;
  188:         const diff =
  189:           BigInt(perLine.gstAmount.minorUnits) - BigInt(invoice.gstAmount.minorUnits);
  190:         const bound = BigInt(items.length);
  191:         expect(diff <= bound && diff >= -bound).toBe(true);
  192:       }),
  193:     );
  194:   });
  195: });
  196: 
  197: describe("determinism and integrity", () => {
  198:   it("same request produces byte-identical results including hashes", async () => {
  199:     const request = makeRequest({ mode: "add", amount: aud("123456") });
  200:     const context = { now: "2026-08-20T10:00:00+08:00" };
  201:     const a = await calculateGst(request, resolution, context);
  202:     const b = await calculateGst(request, resolution, context);
  203:     expect(JSON.stringify(a)).toBe(JSON.stringify(b));
  204:     expect(a.integrity.canonicalResultHash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  205:   });
  206:

  210:     });

  212:     expect(rateAssumption?.category).toBe("official_rule");
  213:     expect(r.sources[0]?.url).toContain("ato.gov.au");
  214:   });
  215: });
### packages/engine-business/src/gst/golden.test.ts
    1: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

    6: import { computeGst, type GstResolution } from "./engine";
    7: import type { GstInput } from "./schema";
    8: 
    9: const fixture = zGoldenFixture.parse(fixtureJson);
   10: const { verified, unverified } = splitGoldenCases(fixture);
   11: 
   12: const resolution: GstResolution = {
   13:   pack: gstPack,
   14:   manifestRef: {
   15:     rulePackId: gstPack.rulePackId,
   16:     rulesVersion: gstPack.rulesVersion,

   36: describe(`golden: ${fixture.fixtureId}`, () => {

   40:     it(`[unverified â€” awaiting owner] ${goldenCase.caseId} executes cleanly`, () => {
   41:       const result = computeGst(makeRequest(goldenCase.input), resolution);
   42:       expect(["success", "success_with_warnings"]).toContain(result.status);
   43:       expect(result.reconciliation?.every((r) => r.passed)).toBe(true);
   44:     });

   46:       // Enabled automatically once the owner fills `expected` in the fixture.
   47:     });
   48:   }
   49: 
   50:   for (const goldenCase of verified) {
   51:     it(`[verified golden] ${goldenCase.caseId}`, () => {
   52:       const result = computeGst(makeRequest(goldenCase.input), resolution);
   53:       const expected = goldenCase.expected as Record<string, unknown>;

   56:         expect(
   57:           (output[key] as { minorUnits?: string })?.minorUnits ?? output[key],
   58:           key,
   59:         ).toEqual(value);
   60:       }
   61:     });
   62:   }
   63: 
   64:   it("sanity: fixture inputs parse and money uses minor-unit strings", () => {
   65:     expect(fixture.cases.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
   66:     expect(() => moneyFromMinorUnits("AUD", "10000", 2)).not.toThrow();
   67:   });
   68: });
### packages/scenario-schema/src/url-state.ts
    1: /**
    2:  * Versioned URL-encoded scenario state (Merge Record #5).
    3:  * Format: "1." + base64url(UTF-8 JSON of { calculatorId, input, jurisdiction?, fy? }).
    4:  * Only calculator input state travels in the URL â€” never results, never
    5:  * identifiers. Decoding an unknown version fails explicitly so future formats
    6:  * can migrate rather than misparse.
    7:  */
    8: export interface UrlScenarioStateV1 {
    9:   calculatorId: string;
   10:   input: unknown;
   11:   subdivision?: string;
   12:   financialYear?: string;
   13: }
   14: 
   15: const VERSION_PREFIX = "1.";
   16: 
   17: export function encodeUrlState(state: UrlScenarioStateV1): string {
   18:   const json = JSON.stringify(state);
   19:   const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(json);
   20:   let binary = "";
   21:   for (const byte of bytes) binary += String.fromCharCode(byte);
   22:   const base64 = btoa(binary).replaceAll("+", "-").replaceAll("/", "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
   23:   return VERSION_PREFIX + base64;
   24: }
   25: 
   26: export type UrlStateDecodeResult =
   27:   | { ok: true; state: UrlScenarioStateV1 }
   28:   | { ok: false; reason: "unsupported_version" | "malformed" };
   29: 
   30: export function decodeUrlState(param: string): UrlStateDecodeResult {
   31:   if (!param.startsWith(VERSION_PREFIX)) return { ok: false, reason: "unsupported_version" };
   32:   const base64 = param.slice(VERSION_PREFIX.length).replaceAll("-", "+").replaceAll("_", "/");
   33:   try {
   34:     const binary = atob(base64);
   35:     const bytes = Uint8Array.from(binary, (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   36:     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));
   37:     if (
   38:       typeof parsed !== "object" ||
   39:       parsed === null ||
   40:       typeof (parsed as { calculatorId?: unknown }).calculatorId !== "string"
   41:     ) {
   42:       return { ok: false, reason: "malformed" };
   43:     }
   44:     return { ok: true, state: parsed as UrlScenarioStateV1 };
   45:   } catch {
   46:     return { ok: false, reason: "malformed" };
   47:   }
   48: }
### apps/web/lib/scenario-store.ts
    1: import type { ScenarioDocumentV1 } from "@paymentcalcs/scenario-schema";
    2: import { zScenarioDocumentV1 } from "@paymentcalcs/scenario-schema";
    3: 
    4: /**
    5:  * Anonymous save (Â§9.6): IndexedDB, no accounts, no server. Values never leave
    6:  * the device. Clearing site data deletes scenarios â€” communicated in UI copy.
    7:  */
    8: const DB_NAME = "paymentcalcs";
    9: const STORE = "scenarios";
   10: 
   11: function openDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
   12:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   13:     const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, 1);
   14:     request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
   15:       const db = request.result;
   16:       if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE)) {
   17:         const store = db.createObjectStore(STORE, { keyPath: "scenarioId" });
   18:         store.createIndex("calculatorId", "calculatorId");
   19:         store.createIndex("updatedAt", "updatedAt");
   20:       }
   21:     };
   22:     request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
   23:     request.onerror = () => reject(request.error ?? new Error("IndexedDB unavailable"));
   24:   });
   25: }
   26: 
   27: function tx<T>(mode: IDBTransactionMode, run: (store: IDBObjectStore) => IDBRequest<T>): Promise<T> {
   28:   return openDb().then(
   29:     (db) =>
   30:       new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
   31:         const transaction = db.transaction(STORE, mode);
   32:         const request = run(transaction.objectStore(STORE));
   33:         request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
   34:         request.onerror = () => reject(request.error ?? new Error("IndexedDB request failed"));
   35:         transaction.oncomplete = () => db.close();
   36:       }),
   37:   );
   38: }
   39: 
   40: export async function saveScenario(document: ScenarioDocumentV1): Promise<void> {
   41:   zScenarioDocumentV1.parse(document);
   42:   await tx("readwrite", (store) => store.put(document));
   43: }
   44: 
   45: export async function listScenarios(calculatorId: string): Promise<ScenarioDocumentV1[]> {
   46:   const all = await tx<unknown[]>("readonly", (store) =>
   47:     store.index("calculatorId").getAll(calculatorId),
   48:   );
   49:   return all
   50:     .map((raw) => zScenarioDocumentV1.safeParse(raw))
   51:     .filter((parsed): parsed is { success: true; data: ScenarioDocumentV1 } => parsed.success)
   52:     .map((parsed) => parsed.data)
   53:     .sort((a, b) => (a.updatedAt < b.updatedAt ? 1 : -1));
   54: }
   55: 
   56: export async function deleteScenario(scenarioId: string): Promise<void> {
   57:   await tx("readwrite", (store) => store.delete(scenarioId));
   58: }

   68: function todayIso(): string {

   71: 
   72: export function GstCalculator() {
   73:   const [resolution, setResolution] = useState<ResolveOutcome | "pending">("pending");
   74:   const [uiMode, setUiMode] = useState<UiMode>("simple");
   75:   const [simpleMode, setSimpleMode] = useState<SimpleMode>("add");
   76:   const [amountRaw, setAmountRaw] = useState("");
   77:   const [items, setItems] = useState<DraftLineItem[]>([emptyLine("line-1")]);
   78:   const [roundingLevel, setRoundingLevel] = useState<"per_line" | "invoice_total">("per_line");
   79:   const [result, setResult] = useState<CalculationResultV1<GstOutput> | null>(null);
   80:   const [savedFlash, setSavedFlash] = useState<string | null>(null);
   81:   // URL hydration must complete before the URL-write effect may run, and must
   82:   // run exactly once (StrictMode double-invokes effects in development).
   83:   const [hydrated, setHydrated] = useState(false);
   84:   const hydratedOnce = useRef(false);
   85: 
   86:   // Resolve the rule pack once (fail-closed; Â§16.6).
   87:   useEffect(() => {
   88:     let cancelled = false;
   89:     resolveRulePack(allAuRulePacks, auIntegrityManifest, {
   90:       domain: "gst",
   91:       jurisdiction: "AU",
   92:       valuationDate: todayIso(),
   93:       allowDraftRules,
   94:     }).then((outcome) => {
   95:       if (cancelled) return;
   96:       setResolution(outcome);
   97:       if (!outcome.ok) {
   98:         analytics.track("rule_unavailable_shown", {
   99:           calculator_id: entry.id,
  100:           rule_status: "unavailable",
  101:         });
  102:       }
  103:     });
  104:     return () => {
  105:       cancelled = true;
  106:     };
  107:   }, []);
  108: 
  109:   // Hydrate input state from the share URL (versioned; unknown versions ignored).
  110:   useEffect(() => {
  111:     if (hydratedOnce.current) return;
  112:     hydratedOnce.current = true;
  113:     const param = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("s");
  114:     if (!param) {
  115:       setHydrated(true);
  116:       return;
  117:     }
  118:     const decoded = decodeUrlState(param);
  119:     if (decoded.ok && decoded.state.calculatorId === entry.id) {
  120:       const input = decoded.state.input as Partial<UrlInputState>;
  121:       if (input.uiMode === "simple" || input.uiMode === "advanced") setUiMode(input.uiMode);
  122:       if (input.simpleMode && ["add", "remove", "split"].includes(input.simpleMode)) {
  123:         setSimpleMode(input.simpleMode);
  124:       }
  125:       if (typeof input.amountRaw === "string") setAmountRaw(input.amountRaw.slice(0, 24));
  126:       if (Array.isArray(input.items) && input.items.length > 0) {
  127:         setItems(input.items.slice(0, 50).map((item, i) => ({ ...emptyLine(`line-${i + 1}`), ...item, id: `line-${i + 1}` })));
  128:       }
  129:       if (input.roundingLevel === "per_line" || input.roundingLevel === "invoice_total") {
  130:         setRoundingLevel(input.roundingLevel);
  131:       }
  132:     }
  133:     setHydrated(true);
  134:   }, []);
  135: 
  136:   // Parse current inputs into an engine input (or field errors).
  137:   const parsed = useMemo(() => {
  138:     if (uiMode === "simple") {
  139:       const money = parseMoneyInput(amountRaw);
  140:       if (!money.ok) {
  141:         return { input: null, amountError: money.error ?? undefined, itemErrors: {} as Record<string, string> };
  142:       }
  143:       return {
  144:         input: { mode: simpleMode, amount: money.money } satisfies GstInput,
  145:         amountError: undefined,
  146:         itemErrors: {},
  147:       };
  148:     }
  149:     const itemErrors: Record<string, string> = {};
  150:     const engineItems: Array<{
  151:       id: string;
  152:       label?: string;
  153:       amount: Money;
  154:       amountIs: "exclusive" | "inclusive";
  155:       treatment: DraftLineItem["treatment"];
  156:       quantity: number;
  157:     }> = [];
  158:     for (const item of items) {
  159:       const money = parseMoneyInput(item.amountRaw);
  160:       const quantity = Number.parseInt(item.quantityRaw, 10);
  161:       if (!money.ok) {
  162:         if (money.error) itemErrors[item.id] = money.error;
  163:         continue;
  164:       }
  165:       if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 1) {
  166:         itemErrors[item.id] = "Quantity must be a whole number of at least 1.";
  167:         continue;
  168:       }
  169:       engineItems.push({
  170:         id: item.id,
  171:         ...(item.label.trim() ? { label: item.label.trim() } : {}),
  172:         amount: money.money,
  173:         amountIs: item.amountIs,
  174:         treatment: item.treatment,
  175:         quantity,
  176:       });
  177:     }
  178:     if (engineItems.length === 0 || Object.keys(itemErrors).length > 0) {
  179:       return { input: null, amountError: undefined, itemErrors };
  180:     }
  181:     return {
  182:       input: { mode: "line_items", items: engineItems, roundingLevel } satisfies GstInput,
  183:       amountError: undefined,
  184:       itemErrors,
  185:     };
  186:   }, [uiMode, simpleMode, amountRaw, items, roundingLevel]);
  187: 
  188:   // Run the engine whenever inputs are valid (Class A â€” sub-millisecond, no loader Â§20.12).
  189:   useEffect(() => {
  190:     if (resolution === "pending" || !resolution.ok || !parsed.input) {
  191:       setResult(null);
  192:       return;
  193:     }
  194:     let cancelled = false;
  195:     const gstResolution: GstResolution = {
  196:       pack: resolution.pack as GstRulePack,
  197:       manifestRef: resolution.manifestRef,
  198:     };
  199:     const started = performance.now();
  200:     calculateGst(
  201:       {
  202:         requestId: "web-live",
  203:         calculatorId: entry.id,
  204:         calculatorSchemaVersion: entry.inputSchemaVersion,
  205:         jurisdiction: { country: "AU" },
  206:         locale: "en-AU",
  207:         currency: "AUD",
  208:         valuationDate: todayIso(),
  209:         input: parsed.input,
  210:         options: { traceLevel: "full" },
  211:       },
  212:       gstResolution,
  213:       { now: new Date().toISOString() },
  214:     ).then((calculated) => {
  215:       if (cancelled) return;
  216:       setResult(calculated);
  217:       analytics.track("calculation_completed", {
  218:         calculator_id: entry.id,
  219:         mode: uiMode === "simple" ? "simple" : "advanced",
  220:         has_warnings: calculated.warnings.length > 0,
  221:         duration_bucket: performance.now() - started < 100 ? "under_100ms" : "under_750ms",
  222:       });
  223:     });
  224:     return () => {
  225:       cancelled = true;
  226:     };
  227:   }, [resolution, parsed.input, uiMode]);
  228: 
  229:   // Reflect input state into the share URL (replace, never push).
  230:   useEffect(() => {
  231:     if (!hydrated) return;
  232:     const state: UrlInputState = {
  233:       uiMode,
  234:       simpleMode,
  235:       amountRaw,
  236:       ...(uiMode === "advanced" ? { items, roundingLevel } : {}),
  237:     };
  238:     const encoded = encodeUrlState({ calculatorId: entry.id, input: state });
  239:     const url = new URL(window.location.href);
  240:     url.searchParams.set("s", encoded);
  241:     window.history.replaceState(null, "", url);
  242:   }, [hydrated, uiMode, simpleMode, amountRaw, items, roundingLevel]);
  243: 
  244:   async function onSave() {
  245:     const now = new Date().toISOString();
  246:     const scenarioId = `sc_${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  247:     try {
  248:       await saveScenario({
  249:         scenarioId,
  250:         schemaVersion: "1",
  251:         calculatorId: entry.id,
  252:         createdAt: now,
  253:         updatedAt: now,
  254:         jurisdiction: { country: "AU" },
  255:         locale: "en-AU",
  256:         currency: "AUD",
  257:         input: { uiMode, simpleMode, amountRaw, items, roundingLevel },
  258:         selectedRulePacks: resolution !== "pending" && resolution.ok ? [resolution.pack.rulePackId] : [],
  259:         consent: { storage: "local" },
  260:       });
  261:       setSavedFlash("Saved on this device");
  262:       analytics.track("scenario_action", { calculator_id: entry.id, action: "save" });
  263:     } catch {
  264:       setSavedFlash("Saving is unavailable in this browser");
  265:     }
  266:     setTimeout(() => setSavedFlash(null), 2500);
  267:   }
  268: 
  269:   async function onShare() {
  270:     try {
  271:       await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
  272:       setSavedFlash("Link copied");
  273:       analytics.track("scenario_action", { calculator_id: entry.id, action: "share" });
  274:     } catch {
  275:       setSavedFlash("Copy the address bar URL to share");
  276:     }
  277:     setTimeout(() => setSavedFlash(null), 2500);
  278:   }

  287:   }
  288:

  500:                             {formatMoney(output.gstAmount)}
  501:                           </td>
  502:                           <td className="py-2 text-right font-mono text-[13px] tabular-nums text-ink">
  503:                             {formatMoney(output.inclusiveAmount)}
  504:                           </td>
  505:                         </tr>
  506:                       </tbody>
  507:                     </table>
  508:                   </div>
  509:                 ) : (
  510:                   <dl className="grid max-w-sm gap-2">
  511:                     {(
  512:                       [
  513:                         ["Excluding GST", output.exclusiveAmount],
  514:                         ["GST component", output.gstAmount],
  515:                         ["Including GST", output.inclusiveAmount],
  516:                       ] as const
  517:                     ).map(([label, amount]) => (
  518:                       <div key={label} className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-6 border-b border-hairline pb-2">
  519:                         <dt className="text-[13px] text-ink-2">{label}</dt>
  520:                         <dd className="font-mono text-[14px] tabular-nums text-ink">{formatMoney(amount)}</dd>
  521:                       </div>
  522:                     ))}
  523:                   </dl>
  524:                 )
  525:               }
  526:               limitations={LIMITATIONS}
  527:             />
  528:           ) : null
  529:         }
  530:         disclosure={<UniversalDisclosure financialYear="current" />}
  531:       />
  532:     </>
  533:   );
  534: }

### packages/engine-business/src/gst/schema.ts
    1: import { z } from "zod";
    2: import { zMoney } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    3: import type { Money } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    4: 
    5: /**
    6:  * AU-BIZ-001 input, schema version 1 (Â§12.15).
    7:  * GST treatment is always explicit â€” never inferred from item labels.
    8:  */
    9: export const GST_TREATMENTS = ["taxable", "gst_free", "input_taxed"] as const;
   10: export type GstTreatment = (typeof GST_TREATMENTS)[number];
   11: 
   12: export const zGstLineItem = z.object({
   13:   id: z.string().min(1),
   14:   label: z.string().max(120).optional(),
   15:   amount: zMoney,
   16:   amountIs: z.enum(["exclusive", "inclusive"]),
   17:   treatment: z.enum(GST_TREATMENTS),
   18:   quantity: z.number().int().min(1).max(9999).default(1),
   19: });
   20: 
   21: export const zGstInput = z.discriminatedUnion("mode", [
   22:   z.object({ mode: z.literal("add"), amount: zMoney }),
   23:   z.object({ mode: z.literal("remove"), amount: zMoney }),
   24:   z.object({ mode: z.literal("split"), amount: zMoney }),
   25:   z.object({
   26:     mode: z.literal("line_items"),
   27:     items: z.array(zGstLineItem).min(1).max(200),
   28:     roundingLevel: z.enum(["per_line", "invoice_total"]),
   29:   }),
   30: ]);
   31: 
   32: export type GstInput = z.infer<typeof zGstInput>;
   33: export type GstLineItem = z.infer<typeof zGstLineItem>;
   34: 
   35: export interface GstLineResult {
   36:   id: string;
   37:   label?: string;
   38:   treatment: GstTreatment;
   39:   quantity: number;
   40:   exclusiveAmount: Money;
   41:   gstAmount: Money;
   42:   inclusiveAmount: Money;
   43: }
   44:

   47:   /** Dimensionless decimal string from the rule pack, e.g. "0.10". */
   48:   rate: string;
   49:   exclusiveAmount: Money;
   50:   gstAmount: Money;
   51:   inclusiveAmount: Money;
   52:   roundingLevel?: "per_line" | "invoice_total";

   54: }
### packages/scenario-schema/src/url-state.ts
    1: /**
    2:  * Versioned URL-encoded scenario state (Merge Record #5).
    3:  * Format: "1." + base64url(UTF-8 JSON of { calculatorId, input, jurisdiction?, fy? }).
    4:  * Only calculator input state travels in the URL â€” never results, never
    5:  * identifiers. Decoding an unknown version fails explicitly so future formats
    6:  * can migrate rather than misparse.
    7:  */
    8: export interface UrlScenarioStateV1 {
    9:   calculatorId: string;
   10:   input: unknown;
   11:   subdivision?: string;
   12:   financialYear?: string;
   13: }
   14: 
   15: const VERSION_PREFIX = "1.";
   16: 
   17: export function encodeUrlState(state: UrlScenarioStateV1): string {
   18:   const json = JSON.stringify(state);
   19:   const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(json);
   20:   let binary = "";
   21:   for (const byte of bytes) binary += String.fromCharCode(byte);
   22:   const base64 = btoa(binary).replaceAll("+", "-").replaceAll("/", "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
   23:   return VERSION_PREFIX + base64;
   24: }
   25: 
   26: export type UrlStateDecodeResult =
   27:   | { ok: true; state: UrlScenarioStateV1 }
   28:   | { ok: false; reason: "unsupported_version" | "malformed" };
   29: 
   30: export function decodeUrlState(param: string): UrlStateDecodeResult {
   31:   if (!param.startsWith(VERSION_PREFIX)) return { ok: false, reason: "unsupported_version" };
   32:   const base64 = param.slice(VERSION_PREFIX.length).replaceAll("-", "+").replaceAll("_", "/");
   33:   try {
   34:     const binary = atob(base64);
   35:     const bytes = Uint8Array.from(binary, (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
   36:     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));
   37:     if (
   38:       typeof parsed !== "object" ||
   39:       parsed === null ||
   40:       typeof (parsed as { calculatorId?: unknown }).calculatorId !== "string"
   41:     ) {
   42:       return { ok: false, reason: "malformed" };
   43:     }
   44:     return { ok: true, state: parsed as UrlScenarioStateV1 };
   45:   } catch {
   46:     return { ok: false, reason: "malformed" };
   47:   }
   48: }
### apps/web/lib/scenario-store.ts
    1: import type { ScenarioDocumentV1 } from "@paymentcalcs/scenario-schema";
    2: import { zScenarioDocumentV1 } from "@paymentcalcs/scenario-schema";
    3: 
    4: /**
    5:  * Anonymous save (Â§9.6): IndexedDB, no accounts, no server. Values never leave
    6:  * the device. Clearing site data deletes scenarios â€” communicated in UI copy.
    7:  */
    8: const DB_NAME = "paymentcalcs";
    9: const STORE = "scenarios";
   10: 
   11: function openDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
   12:   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   13:     const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, 1);
   14:     request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
   15:       const db = request.result;
   16:       if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE)) {
   17:         const store = db.createObjectStore(STORE, { keyPath: "scenarioId" });
   18:         store.createIndex("calculatorId", "calculatorId");
   19:         store.createIndex("updatedAt", "updatedAt");
   20:       }
   21:     };
   22:     request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
   23:     request.onerror = () => reject(request.error ?? new Error("IndexedDB unavailable"));
   24:   });
   25: }
   26: 
   27: function tx<T>(mode: IDBTransactionMode, run: (store: IDBObjectStore) => IDBRequest<T>): Promise<T> {
   28:   return openDb().then(
   29:     (db) =>
   30:       new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
   31:         const transaction = db.transaction(STORE, mode);
   32:         const request = run(transaction.objectStore(STORE));
   33:         request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
   34:         request.onerror = () => reject(request.error ?? new Error("IndexedDB request failed"));
   35:         transaction.oncomplete = () => db.close();
   36:       }),
   37:   );
   38: }
   39: 
   40: export async function saveScenario(document: ScenarioDocumentV1): Promise<void> {
   41:   zScenarioDocumentV1.parse(document);
   42:   await tx("readwrite", (store) => store.put(document));
   43: }
   44: 
   45: export async function listScenarios(calculatorId: string): Promise<ScenarioDocumentV1[]> {
   46:   const all = await tx<unknown[]>("readonly", (store) =>
   47:     store.index("calculatorId").getAll(calculatorId),
   48:   );
   49:   return all
   50:     .map((raw) => zScenarioDocumentV1.safeParse(raw))
   51:     .filter((parsed): parsed is { success: true; data: ScenarioDocumentV1 } => parsed.success)
   52:     .map((parsed) => parsed.data)
   53:     .sort((a, b) => (a.updatedAt < b.updatedAt ? 1 : -1));
   54: }
   55: 
   56: export async function deleteScenario(scenarioId: string): Promise<void> {
   57:   await tx("readwrite", (store) => store.delete(scenarioId));
   58: }

   40: 
   41: interface UrlInputState {

   45:   items?: DraftLineItem[];

   48: 
   49: const SIMPLE_MODES: Array<{ value: SimpleMode; label: string }> = [
   50:   { value: "add", label: "Add GST" },
   51:   { value: "remove", label: "Remove GST" },
   52:   { value: "split", label: "Split GST" },
   53: ];

   56:   add: "GST-inclusive total",

   59: };

   62:   "This tool works with amounts you enter and the standard GST rate only. It does not determine whether you are or must be registered for GST.",
   63:   "Whether a supply is taxable, GST-free or input taxed is your selection â€” the calculator never infers it from item names, and entitlement to input tax credits is not assessed.",
   64:   "Special GST rules (margin scheme, reverse charge, imports, long-term accommodation) are out of scope for this calculator.",
   65:   "Result accuracy class A: deterministic arithmetic on your inputs under the resolved rule pack.",
   66: ];
   67: 
   68: function todayIso(): string {
   69:   return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
   70: }
   71: 
   72: export function GstCalculator() {
   73:   const [resolution, setResolution] = useState<ResolveOutcome | "pending">("pending");
   74:   const [uiMode, setUiMode] = useState<UiMode>("simple");
   75:   const [simpleMode, setSimpleMode] = useState<SimpleMode>("add");
   76:   const [amountRaw, setAmountRaw] = useState("");
   77:   const [items, setItems] = useState<DraftLineItem[]>([emptyLine("line-1")]);
   78:   const [roundingLevel, setRoundingLevel] = useState<"per_line" | "invoice_total">("per_line");
   79:   const [result, setResult] = useState<CalculationResultV1<GstOutput> | null>(null);
   80:   const [savedFlash, setSavedFlash] = useState<string | null>(null);
   81:   // URL hydration must complete before the URL-write effect may run, and must
   82:   // run exactly once (StrictMode double-invokes effects in development).
   83:   const [hydrated, setHydrated] = useState(false);
   84:   const hydratedOnce = useRef(false);
   85: 
   86:   // Resolve the rule pack once (fail-closed; Â§16.6).
   87:   useEffect(() => {
   88:     let cancelled = false;
   89:     resolveRulePack(allAuRulePacks, auIntegrityManifest, {
   90:       domain: "gst",
   91:       jurisdiction: "AU",
   92:       valuationDate: todayIso(),
   93:       allowDraftRules,
   94:     }).then((outcome) => {
   95:       if (cancelled) return;
   96:       setResolution(outcome);
   97:       if (!outcome.ok) {
   98:         analytics.track("rule_unavailable_shown", {
   99:           calculator_id: entry.id,
  100:           rule_status: "unavailable",
  101:         });
  102:       }
  103:     });
  104:     return () => {
  105:       cancelled = true;
  106:     };
  107:   }, []);
  108: 
  109:   // Hydrate input state from the share URL (versioned; unknown versions ignored).
  110:   useEffect(() => {
  111:     if (hydratedOnce.current) return;
  112:     hydratedOnce.current = true;
  113:     const param = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("s");
  114:     if (!param) {
  115:       setHydrated(true);
  116:       return;
  117:     }
  118:     const decoded = decodeUrlState(param);
  119:     if (decoded.ok && decoded.state.calculatorId === entry.id) {
  120:       const input = decoded.state.input as Partial<UrlInputState>;
  121:       if (input.uiMode === "simple" || input.uiMode === "advanced") setUiMode(input.uiMode);
  122:       if (input.simpleMode && ["add", "remove", "split"].includes(input.simpleMode)) {
  123:         setSimpleMode(input.simpleMode);
  124:       }
  125:       if (typeof input.amountRaw === "string") setAmountRaw(input.amountRaw.slice(0, 24));
  126:       if (Array.isArray(input.items) && input.items.length > 0) {
  127:         setItems(input.items.slice(0, 50).map((item, i) => ({ ...emptyLine(`line-${i + 1}`), ...item, id: `line-${i + 1}` })));
  128:       }
  129:       if (input.roundingLevel === "per_line" || input.roundingLevel === "invoice_total") {
  130:         setRoundingLevel(input.roundingLevel);
  131:       }
  132:     }
  133:     setHydrated(true);
  134:   }, []);
  135: 
  136:   // Parse current inputs into an engine input (or field errors).
  137:   const parsed = useMemo(() => {
  138:     if (uiMode === "simple") {
  139:       const money = parseMoneyInput(amountRaw);
  140:       if (!money.ok) {
  141:         return { input: null, amountError: money.error ?? undefined, itemErrors: {} as Record<string, string> };
  142:       }
  143:       return {
  144:         input: { mode: simpleMode, amount: money.money } satisfies GstInput,
  145:         amountError: undefined,
  146:         itemErrors: {},
  147:       };
  148:     }
  149:     const itemErrors: Record<string, string> = {};
  150:     const engineItems: Array<{
  151:       id: string;
  152:       label?: string;
  153:       amount: Money;
  154:       amountIs: "exclusive" | "inclusive";
  155:       treatment: DraftLineItem["treatment"];
  156:       quantity: number;

The first concrete failure is in invoice-total allocation: an adjustment applied to an inclusive-priced final line rewrites the customer-entered gross amount. I’m checking adjacent schemas and call paths now for defects that can mask or compound it.

   17:     minorUnits: z.string().regex(/^-?(0|[1-9]\d*)$/),
   18:     scale: z.number().int().min(0).max(12),
   19:   })
   20:   .transform((raw) => moneyFromMinorUnits(raw.currency, raw.minorUnits, raw.scale));

### packages/calculation-core/src/money.ts
    1: import { Dec, dec, type DecimalString, type DecimalValue } from "./decimal";
    2: import { roundTo, type RoundingMode } from "./rounding";
    3: 
    4: export type CurrencyCode = string;
    5: 
    6: declare const moneyBrand: unique symbol;
    7: 
    8: /**
    9:  * Â§14.1 â€” money is an integer count of minor units serialised as a string.
   10:  * The brand prevents constructing Money from arbitrary object literals and,
   11:  * together with the engine lint rule, keeps `number` out of currency paths.
   12:  */
   13: export interface Money {
   14:   readonly currency: CurrencyCode;
   15:   /** Signed arbitrary-size integer serialised as a string. */
   16:   readonly minorUnits: string;
   17:   /** Number of minor-unit digits after the major-unit point (AUD: 2). */
   18:   readonly scale: number;
   19:   readonly [moneyBrand]: true;
   20: }
   21: 
   22: const INTEGER_STRING = /^-?(0|[1-9]\d*)$/;
   23: 
   24: export class MoneyDomainError extends Error {
   25:   readonly code = "PC-VAL-0001";
   26: }
   27: 
   28: export function moneyFromMinorUnits(
   29:   currency: CurrencyCode,
   30:   minorUnits: string,
   31:   scale: number,
   32: ): Money {
   33:   if (!INTEGER_STRING.test(minorUnits)) {
   34:     throw new MoneyDomainError(`minorUnits must be a signed integer string, got "${minorUnits}"`);
   35:   }
   36:   if (!Number.isInteger(scale) || scale < 0 || scale > 12) {
   37:     throw new MoneyDomainError(`scale must be an integer in [0,12], got ${scale}`);
   38:   }
   39:   if (!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(currency)) {
   40:     throw new MoneyDomainError(`currency must be an ISO 4217 code, got "${currency}"`);
   41:   }
   42:   return { currency, minorUnits: normaliseZero(minorUnits), scale } as Money;
   43: }
   44: 
   45: function normaliseZero(minorUnits: string): string {
   46:   return minorUnits === "-0" ? "0" : minorUnits;
   47: }
   48: 
   49: /** Parse a major-unit decimal string ("1234.56") into Money at the given scale. */
   50: export function moneyFromDecimalString(
   51:   currency: CurrencyCode,
   52:   major: DecimalString,
   53:   scale: number,
   54:   mode: RoundingMode = "half_up",
   55: ): Money {
   56:   const minor = roundTo(dec(major).times(new Dec(10).pow(scale)) as DecimalValue, 0, mode);
   57:   return moneyFromMinorUnits(currency, minor.toFixed(0), scale);
   58: }
   59: 
   60: /** Money as an exact decimal in major units. */
   61: export function moneyToDecimal(money: Money): DecimalValue {
   62:   return dec(money.minorUnits).div(new Dec(10).pow(money.scale)) as DecimalValue;
   63: }
   64: 
   65: /** Serialised major-unit decimal at the money's own scale, e.g. "1234.56". */
   66: export function moneyToDecimalString(money: Money): DecimalString {
   67:   return moneyToDecimal(money).toFixed(money.scale);
   68: }
   69: 
   70: function assertSameDenomination(a: Money, b: Money): void {
   71:   if (a.currency !== b.currency || a.scale !== b.scale) {
   72:     throw new MoneyDomainError(
   73:       `cannot combine ${a.currency}/${a.scale} with ${b.currency}/${b.scale}`,
   74:     );
   75:   }
   76: }
   77: 
   78: export function addMoney(a: Money, b: Money): Money {
   79:   assertSameDenomination(a, b);
   80:   return moneyFromMinorUnits(
   81:     a.currency,
   82:     dec(a.minorUnits).plus(dec(b.minorUnits)).toFixed(0),
   83:     a.scale,
   84:   );
   85: }
   86: 
   87: export function subtractMoney(a: Money, b: Money): Money {
   88:   assertSameDenomination(a, b);
   89:   return moneyFromMinorUnits(
   90:     a.currency,
   91:     dec(a.minorUnits).minus(dec(b.minorUnits)).toFixed(0),
   92:     a.scale,
   93:   );
   94: }
   95: 
   96: export function negateMoney(a: Money): Money {
   97:   return moneyFromMinorUnits(a.currency, dec(a.minorUnits).negated().toFixed(0), a.scale);
   98: }
   99: 
  100: export function sumMoney(currency: CurrencyCode, scale: number, items: readonly Money[]): Money {
  101:   let total = moneyFromMinorUnits(currency, "0", scale);
  102:   for (const item of items) total = addMoney(total, item);
  103:   return total;
  104: }
  105: 
  106: /** Round an exact minor-unit decimal (e.g. 123.456 cents) into Money. */
  107: export function moneyFromDecimalMinorUnits(
  108:   currency: CurrencyCode,
  109:   scale: number,
  110:   exactMinorUnits: DecimalValue,
  111:   mode: RoundingMode,
  112: ): Money {
  113:   return moneyFromMinorUnits(currency, roundTo(exactMinorUnits, 0, mode).toFixed(0), scale);
  114: }
  115: 
  116: /** Multiply by an exact decimal factor, rounding minor units with the given mode. */
  117: export function multiplyMoney(a: Money, factor: DecimalValue, mode: RoundingMode): Money {
  118:   const product = roundTo(dec(a.minorUnits).times(factor) as DecimalValue, 0, mode);
  119:   return moneyFromMinorUnits(a.currency, product.toFixed(0), a.scale);
  120: }
  121: 
  122: export function compareMoney(a: Money, b: Money): -1 | 0 | 1 {
  123:   assertSameDenomination(a, b);
  124:   return dec(a.minorUnits).comparedTo(dec(b.minorUnits)) as -1 | 0 | 1;
  125: }
  126: 
  127: export function isZeroMoney(a: Money): boolean {
  128:   return dec(a.minorUnits).isZero();
  129: }
  130: 
  131: export function absMoney(a: Money): Money {
  132:   return dec(a.minorUnits).isNegative() ? negateMoney(a) : a;
  133: }
  134: 
  135: export function zeroMoney(currency: CurrencyCode, scale: number): Money {
  136:   return moneyFromMinorUnits(currency, "0", scale);
  137: }

   21: 
   22: /** Statuses additionally runnable in dev under PC_ALLOW_DRAFT_RULES (draft banner). */
   23: export const DRAFT_RUNNABLE_STATUSES: readonly RulePackStatus[] = [
   24:   "draft",
   25:   "in_review",
   26:   "approved",
   27:   "scheduled",
   28: ];
   29: 
   30: export interface RulePackV1<TRules = unknown> {
   31:   rulePackId: string;
   32:   jurisdiction: string;
   33:   subdivision: string | null;
   34:   domain: string;
   35:   effectiveFrom: ISODate;
   36:   /** null = open-ended until superseded. */
   37:   effectiveTo: ISODate | null;
   38:   status: RulePackStatus;
   39:   schemaVersion: 1;
   40:   rulesVersion: string;
   41:   sources: SourceRecordV1[];
   42:   review: {
   43:     preparedBy: string;
   44:     approvedBy: string | null;
   45:     approvedAt: ISODateTime | null;
   46:   };
   47:   /** null until a human verifies every value against the cited sources. */
   48:   verifiedAt: ISODateTime | null;
   49:   /** Domain payload. Values may be null when the source could not be fetched. */
   50:   rules: TRules;
   51: }
   52: 
   53: export const zSourceRecordV1: z.ZodType<SourceRecordV1> = z.object({
   54:   sourceId: z.string().min(1),
   55:   authority: z.string().min(1),
   56:   title: z.string().min(1),
   57:   url: z.url(),
   58:   jurisdiction: z.string().min(2),
   59:   domain: z.string().min(1),
   60:   publicationDate: zISODate.optional(),
   61:   effectiveFrom: zISODate.optional(),
   62:   effectiveTo: zISODate.optional(),
   63:   retrievedAt: zISODateTime,
   64:   archivedSnapshotRef: z.string().optional(),
   65:   contentHash: z.string().regex(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/).optional(),
   66:   notes: z.string().optional(),
   67: });
   68: 
   69: export function zRulePackV1<TRules extends z.ZodType>(rules: TRules) {
   70:   return z.object({
   71:     rulePackId: z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+$/),
   72:     jurisdiction: z.string().length(2),
   73:     subdivision: z.string().min(2).nullable(),
   74:     domain: z.string().min(1),
   75:     effectiveFrom: zISODate,
   76:     effectiveTo: zISODate.nullable(),
   77:     status: z.enum(RULE_PACK_STATUSES),
   78:     schemaVersion: z.literal(1),
   79:     rulesVersion: z.string().regex(/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/),
   80:     sources: z.array(zSourceRecordV1).min(1),
   81:     review: z.object({
   82:       preparedBy: z.string().min(1),
   83:       approvedBy: z.string().min(1).nullable(),
   84:       approvedAt: zISODateTime.nullable(),
   85:     }),
   86:     verifiedAt: zISODateTime.nullable(),
   87:     rules,
   88:   });
   89: }
   90: 
   91: export const zRulePackEnvelope = zRulePackV1(z.unknown());
### packages/rule-schema/src/resolver.ts
    1: import type { ISODate, RulePackManifestRef } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    2: import { verifyPackIntegrity, type IntegrityManifest } from "./integrity";
    3: import {
    4:   DRAFT_RUNNABLE_STATUSES,
    5:   RUNNABLE_STATUSES,
    6:   type RulePackStatus,
    7:   type RulePackV1,
    8: } from "./schema";
    9: 
   10: export interface ResolveQuery {
   11:   domain: string;
   12:   jurisdiction: string;
   13:   subdivision?: string | null;
   14:   valuationDate: ISODate;
   15:   /**
   16:    * True only when the build runs with PC_ALLOW_DRAFT_RULES=1 in development.
   17:    * Callers MUST derive this from a build-time guard that cannot be enabled in
   18:    * production bundles; the resolver itself never reads the environment.
   19:    */
   20:   allowDraftRules?: boolean;
   21: }
   22: 
   23: export type ResolveOutcome =
   24:   | {
   25:       ok: true;
   26:       pack: RulePackV1;
   27:       manifestRef: RulePackManifestRef;
   28:       /** True when the pack ran only because draft rules are allowed (dev). */
   29:       draft: boolean;
   30:     }
   31:   | {
   32:       ok: false;
   33:       /** Â§H.6 rule-resolution code. */
   34:       code: "PC-RULE-0001" | "PC-RULE-0002" | "PC-RULE-0003";
   35:       reason: string;
   36:     };
   37: 
   38: function coversDate(pack: RulePackV1, date: ISODate): boolean {
   39:   if (pack.effectiveFrom > date) return false;
   40:   if (pack.effectiveTo !== null && pack.effectiveTo < date) return false;
   41:   return true;
   42: }
   43: 
   44: function statusRunnable(status: RulePackStatus, allowDraft: boolean): boolean {
   45:   if (RUNNABLE_STATUSES.includes(status)) return true;
   46:   return allowDraft && DRAFT_RUNNABLE_STATUSES.includes(status);
   47: }
   48: 
   49: /**
   50:  * Fail-closed rule-pack resolution (Â§16.6, non-negotiable #12).
   51:  * Any failure returns a structured error; callers render the
   52:  * "Rule unavailable" state and MUST NOT substitute another rule set.
   53:  */
   54: export async function resolveRulePack(
   55:   packs: readonly RulePackV1[],
   56:   manifest: IntegrityManifest,
   57:   query: ResolveQuery,
   58: ): Promise<ResolveOutcome> {
   59:   const allowDraft = query.allowDraftRules === true;
   60:   const candidates = packs.filter(
   61:     (pack) =>
   62:       pack.domain === query.domain &&
   63:       pack.jurisdiction === query.jurisdiction &&
   64:       (query.subdivision === undefined || pack.subdivision === (query.subdivision ?? null)) &&
   65:       coversDate(pack, query.valuationDate),
   66:   );
   67: 
   68:   if (candidates.length === 0) {
   69:     return {
   70:       ok: false,
   71:       code: "PC-RULE-0001",
   72:       reason: `no rule pack for domain "${query.domain}" (${query.jurisdiction}) covering ${query.valuationDate}`,
   73:     };
   74:   }
   75: 
   76:   const runnable = candidates.filter((pack) => statusRunnable(pack.status, allowDraft));
   77:   if (runnable.length === 0) {
   78:     return {
   79:       ok: false,
   80:       code: "PC-RULE-0002",
   81:       reason: `rule pack exists but none is active (statuses: ${candidates.map((p) => p.status).join(", ")})`,
   82:     };
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   // Prefer active over draft-runnable; then the most recently effective.
   86:   const chosen = [...runnable].sort((a, b) => {
   87:     const aActive = RUNNABLE_STATUSES.includes(a.status) ? 1 : 0;
   88:     const bActive = RUNNABLE_STATUSES.includes(b.status) ? 1 : 0;
   89:     if (aActive !== bActive) return bActive - aActive;
   90:     return a.effectiveFrom < b.effectiveFrom ? 1 : a.effectiveFrom > b.effectiveFrom ? -1 : 0;
   91:   })[0]!;
   92: 
   93:   const integrity = await verifyPackIntegrity(chosen, manifest);
   94:   if (!integrity.ok) {
   95:     return { ok: false, code: "PC-RULE-0003", reason: integrity.reason };
   96:   }
   97: 
   98:   return {
   99:     ok: true,
  100:     pack: chosen,
  101:     draft: !RUNNABLE_STATUSES.includes(chosen.status),
  102:     manifestRef: {
  103:       rulePackId: chosen.rulePackId,
  104:       rulesVersion: chosen.rulesVersion,
  105:       status: chosen.status,
  106:       integritySha256: integrity.sha256,
  107:     },
  108:   };
  109: }
### packages/rule-schema/src/integrity.ts
    1: import { canonicalHash } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    2: import type { RulePackV1 } from "./schema";
    3: 
    4: /**
    5:  * Integrity manifest: pack ID + rules version â†’ pinned sha256 of the pack's
    6:  * canonical serialisation. The manifest is regenerated only by the explicit
    7:  * `rules:hash` script; any drift between pack content and manifest is treated
    8:  * as integrity failure and the resolver fails closed (Â§16.6).
    9:  */
   10: export type IntegrityManifest = Record<string, string>;
   11: 
   12: export function manifestKey(pack: RulePackV1): string {
   13:   return `${pack.rulePackId}@${pack.rulesVersion}`;
   14: }
   15: 
   16: export async function computePackHash(pack: RulePackV1): Promise<string> {
   17:   return canonicalHash(pack);
   18: }
   19: 
   20: export async function verifyPackIntegrity(
   21:   pack: RulePackV1,
   22:   manifest: IntegrityManifest,
   23: ): Promise<{ ok: true; sha256: string } | { ok: false; reason: string }> {
   24:   const key = manifestKey(pack);
   25:   const pinned = manifest[key];
   26:   if (!pinned) return { ok: false, reason: `no integrity pin for ${key}` };
   27:   const actual = await computePackHash(pack);
   28:   if (actual !== pinned) {
   29:     return { ok: false, reason: `integrity mismatch for ${key}` };
   30:   }
   31:   return { ok: true, sha256: actual };
   32: }

    1: /**
    2:  * Non-negotiable #12: PC_ALLOW_DRAFT_RULES may never take effect in a

    4:  * - local dev (NODE_ENV !== "production"): flag works, DRAFT banner shows;
    5:  * - Vercel *preview* deployments (NEXT_PUBLIC_VERCEL_ENV === "preview",
    6:  *   a system-provided value): flag works, DRAFT banner shows â€” owner-approved
    7:  *   demo surface, see PROGRESS.md D-006;
    8:  * - production deployments (NEXT_PUBLIC_VERCEL_ENV === "production" or any
    9:  *   non-Vercel production build): compiles to false. Impossible to enable.
   10:  */
   11: export const allowDraftRules: boolean =
   12:   process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_PC_ALLOW_DRAFT_RULES === "1" &&
   13:   (process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production" ||
   14:     process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_VERCEL_ENV === "preview");
### apps/web/lib/analytics.ts

    3: /** All product analytics flow through the Â§18.4 redaction wrapper. */

    8:         console.warn(
    9:           `[analytics-safe] dropped "${rejection.event}" (${rejection.reason}${rejection.propertyKey ? `: ${rejection.propertyKey}` : ""})`,
   10:         );

### packages/analytics-safe/src/analytics.ts
    1: import { redactEvent, type AllowedValue, type RejectedEvent } from "./redaction";
    2: 
    3: export interface AnalyticsProvider {
    4:   send(event: string, props: Record<string, AllowedValue>): void;
    5: }
    6: 
    7: export interface Analytics {
    8:   track(event: string, props?: Record<string, unknown>): void;
    9: }
   10: 
   11: /**
   12:  * The only sanctioned path to any analytics provider. Rejected events are
   13:  * dropped (never partially sent) and surfaced to an optional observer that
   14:  * receives keys and reasons only â€” never values.
   15:  */
   16: export function createAnalytics(
   17:   provider: AnalyticsProvider,
   18:   onRejected?: (rejection: RejectedEvent) => void,
   19: ): Analytics {
   20:   return {
   21:     track(event, props = {}) {
   22:       const outcome = redactEvent(event, props);
   23:       if (outcome.accepted) {
   24:         provider.send(outcome.event, outcome.props);
   25:       } else {
   26:         onRejected?.(outcome.rejection);
   27:       }
   28:     },
   29:   };
   30: }
   31: 
   32: /** Plausible script-tag provider (no cookies, no consent banner). */
   33: export function plausibleProvider(): AnalyticsProvider {
   34:   return {
   35:     send(event, props) {
   36:       const w = globalThis as { plausible?: (e: string, o?: { props: unknown }) => void };
   37:       w.plausible?.(event, { props });
   38:     },
   39:   };
   40: }
   41: 
   42: export function noopProvider(): AnalyticsProvider {
   43:   return { send() {} };
   44: }
### packages/analytics-safe/src/redaction.ts
    1: /**
    2:  * Â§18.4 â€” central analytics redaction.
    3:  *
    4:  * Design: strict allowlist, not blocklist. A property is accepted only when its
    5:  * key is registered AND its value passes that key's validator. Everything else
    6:  * â€” money amounts, incomes, balances, rates-as-personal-facts, ages, free text,
    7:  * unknown keys â€” is rejected by construction. Rejection is per-event: one bad
    8:  * property drops the whole event, so partial leakage is impossible.
    9:  */
   10: export type AllowedValue = string | boolean;
   11: 
   12: export interface RejectedEvent {
   13:   event: string;
   14:   reason: string;
   15:   /** The offending KEY only. Values are never echoed anywhere. */
   16:   propertyKey?: string;
   17: }
   18: 
   19: const CALCULATOR_ID = /^(AU|GL)-[A-Z]+-\d{3}$/;
   20: const FINANCIAL_YEAR = /^\d{4}-\d{2}$/;
   21: const DURATION_BUCKET = /^(under_100ms|under_750ms|under_2s|over_2s)$/;
   22: const ERROR_CODE = /^PC-[A-Z]+-\d{4}$/;
   23: 
   24: const ALLOWED_PROPERTIES: Record<string, (value: AllowedValue) => boolean> = {
   25:   calculator_id: (v) => typeof v === "string" && CALCULATOR_ID.test(v),
   26:   mode: (v) => v === "simple" || v === "advanced" || v === "compare",
   27:   financial_year: (v) => typeof v === "string" && FINANCIAL_YEAR.test(v),
   28:   jurisdiction: (v) => typeof v === "string" && /^[A-Z]{2}(-[A-Z]{2,3})?$/.test(v),
   29:   has_warnings: (v) => typeof v === "boolean",
   30:   duration_bucket: (v) => typeof v === "string" && DURATION_BUCKET.test(v),
   31:   error_code: (v) => typeof v === "string" && ERROR_CODE.test(v),
   32:   rule_status: (v) =>
   33:     v === "active" || v === "draft" || v === "unavailable" || v === "historical",
   34:   theme: (v) => v === "light" || v === "dark" || v === "system",
   35:   action: (v) =>
   36:     typeof v === "string" &&
   37:     ["save", "share", "export_csv", "export_json", "print", "reset", "mode_switch"].includes(v),
   38: };
   39: 
   40: const ALLOWED_EVENTS = [
   41:   "page_view",
   42:   "calculation_completed",
   43:   "calculation_failed",
   44:   "rule_unavailable_shown",
   45:   "scenario_action",
   46:   "explainability_opened",
   47:   "disclosure_viewed",
   48: ] as const;
   49: 
   50: export type AllowedEventName = (typeof ALLOWED_EVENTS)[number];
   51: 
   52: export type RedactionOutcome =
   53:   | { accepted: true; event: string; props: Record<string, AllowedValue> }
   54:   | { accepted: false; rejection: RejectedEvent };
   55: 
   56: export function redactEvent(event: string, props: Record<string, unknown>): RedactionOutcome {
   57:   if (!(ALLOWED_EVENTS as readonly string[]).includes(event)) {
   58:     return { accepted: false, rejection: { event, reason: "event_not_allowlisted" } };
   59:   }
   60:   const clean: Record<string, AllowedValue> = {};
   61:   for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(props)) {
   62:     const validator = ALLOWED_PROPERTIES[key];
   63:     if (!validator) {
   64:       return {
   65:         accepted: false,
   66:         rejection: { event, reason: "property_not_allowlisted", propertyKey: key },
   67:       };
   68:     }
   69:     if (typeof value !== "string" && typeof value !== "boolean") {
   70:       // Numbers are rejected wholesale: every Â§18.4-forbidden class (amounts,
   71:       // incomes, balances, ages) arrives as a number or numeric string.
   72:       return {
   73:         accepted: false,
   74:         rejection: { event, reason: "value_type_forbidden", propertyKey: key },
   75:       };
   76:     }
   77:     if (!validator(value)) {
   78:       return {
   79:         accepted: false,
   80:         rejection: { event, reason: "value_failed_validation", propertyKey: key },
   81:       };
   82:     }
   83:     clean[key] = value;
   84:   }
   85:   return { accepted: true, event, props: clean };
   86: }
