### packages/engine-business/src/gst/engine.test.ts
    1: import fc from "fast-check";
    2: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
    3: import {
    4:   addMoney,
    5:   compareMoney,
    6:   moneyFromMinorUnits,
    7:   type CalculationRequestV1,
    8: } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    9: import { gstPack } from "@paymentcalcs/rules-au";
   10: import { calculateGst, computeGst, type GstResolution } from "./engine";
   11: import type { GstInput } from "./schema";
   12: 
   13: const aud = (minor: string) => moneyFromMinorUnits("AUD", minor, 2);
   14: 
   15: const resolution: GstResolution = {
   16:   pack: gstPack,
   17:   manifestRef: {
   18:     rulePackId: gstPack.rulePackId,
   19:     rulesVersion: gstPack.rulesVersion,
   20:     status: gstPack.status,
   21:     integritySha256: "0".repeat(64),
   22:   },
   23: };
   24: 
   25: function makeRequest(input: GstInput): CalculationRequestV1<GstInput> {
   26:   return {
   27:     requestId: "req-test",
   28:     calculatorId: "AU-BIZ-001",
   29:     calculatorSchemaVersion: "1",
   30:     jurisdiction: { country: "AU" },
   31:     locale: "en-AU",
   32:     currency: "AUD",
   33:     valuationDate: "2026-08-20",
   34:     input,
   35:     options: { traceLevel: "full" },
   36:   };
   37: }
   38: 
   39: function run(input: GstInput) {
   40:   return computeGst(makeRequest(input), resolution);
   41: }
   42: 
   43: describe("GST boundaries (Â§12.15 acceptance criteria)", () => {
   44:   it("adds GST to $100.00 exactly", () => {
   45:     const r = run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("10000") });
   46:     expect(r.status).toBe("success");
   47:     expect(r.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("1000");
   48:     expect(r.output?.inclusiveAmount.minorUnits).toBe("11000");
   49:   });
   50: 
   51:   it("handles zero and one-cent amounts without error", () => {
   52:     expect(run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("0") }).output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("0");
   53:     const oneCent = run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("1") });
   54:     // 10% of 1c = 0.1c â†’ rounds half-up to 0c.
   55:     expect(oneCent.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("0");
   56:     expect(run({ mode: "split", amount: aud("1") }).output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("0");
   57:   });
   58: 
   59:   it("rounds the half-cent boundary half-up: 10% of $0.05 = 1c", () => {
   60:     const r = run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("5") });
   61:     expect(r.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("1");
   62:   });
   63: 
   64:   it("splits a GST-inclusive $110.00 into $100.00 + $10.00", () => {
   65:     const r = run({ mode: "split", amount: aud("11000") });
   66:     expect(r.output?.exclusiveAmount.minorUnits).toBe("10000");
   67:     expect(r.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("1000");
   68:   });
   69: 
   70:   it("flags negative amounts as credits with a warning, still calculating", () => {
   71:     const r = run({ mode: "add", amount: aud("-10000") });
   72:     expect(r.status).toBe("success_with_warnings");
   73:     expect(r.output?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("-1000");
   74:   });
   75: 
   76:   it("never infers treatment from labels: a line labelled 'milk' is taxed when marked taxable", () => {
   77:     const r = run({
   78:       mode: "line_items",
   79:       roundingLevel: "per_line",
   80:       items: [
   81:         { id: "l1", label: "milk (GST-free in real life)", amount: aud("1000"), amountIs: "exclusive", treatment: "taxable", quantity: 1 },
   82:         { id: "l2", label: "consulting", amount: aud("1000"), amountIs: "exclusive", treatment: "gst_free", quantity: 1 },
   83:       ],
   84:     });
   85:     expect(r.output?.lines?.[0]?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("100");
   86:     expect(r.output?.lines?.[1]?.gstAmount.minorUnits).toBe("0");
   87:   });
   88: 
   89:   it("fails closed when the pack rate is null", () => {
   90:     const nullPack = { ...gstPack, rules: { standardRate: null } };
   91:     const r = computeGst(makeRequest({ mode: "add", amount: aud("100") }), {
   92:       ...resolution,
   93:       pack: nullPack,
   94:     });
   95:     expect(r.status).toBe("failed");
   96:     expect(r.errors[0]?.code).toBe("PC-RULE-0004");
   97:     expect(r.output).toBeUndefined();
   98:   });
   99: 
  100:   it("rejects malformed input as invalid, not thrown", () => {
  101:     const r = computeGst(
  102:       makeRequest({ mode: "add", amount: { bad: true } } as unknown as GstInput),
  103:       resolution,
  104:     );
  105:     expect(r.status).toBe("invalid");
  106:     expect(r.errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  107:   });
  108: });
  109: 
  110: const minorUnitsArb = fc
  111:   .bigInt({ min: -99999999999n, max: 99999999999n })
  112:   .map((n) => aud(n.toString()));
  113: 
  114: describe("GST properties", () => {
  115:   it("reconciliation always passes and is exact: exclusive + gst = inclusive", () => {
  116:     fc.assert(
  117:       fc.property(minorUnitsArb, fc.constantFrom<GstInput["mode"]>("add", "remove", "split"), (amount, mode) => {
  118:         const r = run({ mode, amount } as GstInput);
  119:         expect(r.reconciliation?.[0]?.passed).toBe(true);
  120:         const out = r.output!;
  121:         expect(addMoney(out.exclusiveAmount, out.gstAmount).minorUnits).toBe(
  122:           out.inclusiveAmount.minorUnits,
  123:         );
  124:       }),
  125:     );
  126:   });
  127: 
  128:   it("GST is monotone non-decreasing in the amount", () => {
  129:     fc.assert(
  130:       fc.property(minorUnitsArb, minorUnitsArb, (a, b) => {
  131:         const [lo, hi] = compareMoney(a, b) <= 0 ? [a, b] : [b, a];
  132:         const gstLo = run({ mode: "add", amount: lo }).output!.gstAmount;
  133:         const gstHi = run({ mode: "add", amount: hi }).output!.gstAmount;
  134:         expect(compareMoney(gstLo, gstHi)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
  135:       }),
  136:     );
  137:   });
  138: 
  139:   it("metamorphic: add then split round-trips within one cent", () => {
  140:     fc.assert(
  141:       fc.property(minorUnitsArb, (exclusive) => {
  142:         const added = run({ mode: "add", amount: exclusive }).output!;
  143:         const split = run({ mode: "split", amount: added.inclusiveAmount }).output!;
  144:         const diff = BigInt(split.exclusiveAmount.minorUnits) - BigInt(exclusive.minorUnits);
  145:         expect(diff <= 1n && diff >= -1n).toBe(true);
  146:       }),
  147:     );
  148:   });
  149: 
  150:   it("line items: lines always sum exactly to totals at both rounding levels", () => {
  151:     const itemArb = fc.record({
  152:       id: fc.uuid(),
  153:       amount: fc.bigInt({ min: 1n, max: 9999999n }).map((n) => aud(n.toString())),
  154:       amountIs: fc.constantFrom("exclusive" as const, "inclusive" as const),
  155:       treatment: fc.constantFrom("taxable" as const, "gst_free" as const, "input_taxed" as const),
  156:       quantity: fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 20 }),
  157:     });
  158:     fc.assert(
  159:       fc.property(
  160:         fc.array(itemArb, { minLength: 1, maxLength: 12 }),
  161:         fc.constantFrom("per_line" as const, "invoice_total" as const),
  162:         (items, roundingLevel) => {
  163:           const r = run({ mode: "line_items", items, roundingLevel });
  164:           expect(r.status === "success" || r.status === "success_with_warnings").toBe(true);
  165:           const out = r.output!;
  166:           const sum = (key: "exclusiveAmount" | "gstAmount" | "inclusiveAmount") =>
  167:             out.lines!.reduce((acc, l) => acc + BigInt(l[key].minorUnits), 0n).toString();
  168:           expect(sum("exclusiveAmount")).toBe(out.exclusiveAmount.minorUnits);
  169:           expect(sum("gstAmount")).toBe(out.gstAmount.minorUnits);
  170:           expect(sum("inclusiveAmount")).toBe(out.inclusiveAmount.minorUnits);
  171:         },
  172:       ),
  173:     );
  174:   });
  175: 
  176:   it("per-line vs invoice-total totals differ by at most half a cent per taxable line", () => {
  177:     const itemArb = fc.record({
  178:       id: fc.uuid(),
  179:       amount: fc.bigInt({ min: 1n, max: 999999n }).map((n) => aud(n.toString())),
  180:       amountIs: fc.constantFrom("exclusive" as const, "inclusive" as const),
  181:       treatment: fc.constant("taxable" as const),
  182:       quantity: fc.integer({ min: 1, max: 5 }),
  183:     });
  184:     fc.assert(
  185:       fc.property(fc.array(itemArb, { minLength: 1, maxLength: 10 }), (items) => {
  186:         const perLine = run({ mode: "line_items", items, roundingLevel: "per_line" }).output!;
  187:         const invoice = run({ mode: "line_items", items, roundingLevel: "invoice_total" }).output!;
  188:         const diff =
  189:           BigInt(perLine.gstAmount.minorUnits) - BigInt(invoice.gstAmount.minorUnits);
  190:         const bound = BigInt(items.length);
  191:         expect(diff <= bound && diff >= -bound).toBe(true);
  192:       }),
  193:     );
  194:   });
  195: });
  196: 
  197: describe("determinism and integrity", () => {
  198:   it("same request produces byte-identical results including hashes", async () => {
  199:     const request = makeRequest({ mode: "add", amount: aud("123456") });
  200:     const context = { now: "2026-08-20T10:00:00+08:00" };
  201:     const a = await calculateGst(request, resolution, context);
  202:     const b = await calculateGst(request, resolution, context);
  203:     expect(JSON.stringify(a)).toBe(JSON.stringify(b));
  204:     expect(a.integrity.canonicalResultHash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
  205:   });
  206: 
  207:   it("labels the rate as an official rule sourced from the pack", async () => {
  208:     const r = await calculateGst(makeRequest({ mode: "add", amount: aud("100") }), resolution, {
  209:       now: "2026-08-20T10:00:00+08:00",
  210:     });
  211:     const rateAssumption = r.assumptions.find((a) => a.id === "gst-standard-rate");
  212:     expect(rateAssumption?.category).toBe("official_rule");
  213:     expect(r.sources[0]?.url).toContain("ato.gov.au");
  214:   });
  215: });
### packages/engine-business/src/gst/golden.test.ts
    1: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

    3: import { splitGoldenCases, zGoldenFixture } from "@paymentcalcs/test-fixtures";

    5: import { gstPack } from "@paymentcalcs/rules-au";
    6: import { computeGst, type GstResolution } from "./engine";
    7: import type { GstInput } from "./schema";
    8: 
    9: const fixture = zGoldenFixture.parse(fixtureJson);
   10: const { verified, unverified } = splitGoldenCases(fixture);
   11: 
   12: const resolution: GstResolution = {
   13:   pack: gstPack,
   14:   manifestRef: {
   15:     rulePackId: gstPack.rulePackId,
   16:     rulesVersion: gstPack.rulesVersion,
   17:     status: gstPack.status,
   18:     integritySha256: "0".repeat(64),
   19:   },
   20: };
   21: 
   22: function makeRequest(input: unknown): CalculationRequestV1<GstInput> {
   23:   return {
   24:     requestId: "req-golden",
   25:     calculatorId: fixture.calculatorId,
   26:     calculatorSchemaVersion: "1",
   27:     jurisdiction: { country: "AU" },
   28:     locale: "en-AU",
   29:     currency: "AUD",
   30:     valuationDate: "2026-08-20",

   33:   };
   34: }

   36: describe(`golden: ${fixture.fixtureId}`, () => {
   37:   // Unverified cases still execute â€” the engine must succeed on every scaffold
   38:   // input â€” but expected-value assertions are skipped and counted in CI.
   39:   for (const goldenCase of unverified) {
   40:     it(`[unverified â€” awaiting owner] ${goldenCase.caseId} executes cleanly`, () => {
   41:       const result = computeGst(makeRequest(goldenCase.input), resolution);
   42:       expect(["success", "success_with_warnings"]).toContain(result.status);
   43:       expect(result.reconciliation?.every((r) => r.passed)).toBe(true);
   44:     });
   45:     it.skip(`[golden pending verification] ${goldenCase.caseId}`, () => {
   46:       // Enabled automatically once the owner fills `expected` in the fixture.
   47:     });
   48:   }
   49: 
   50:   for (const goldenCase of verified) {
   51:     it(`[verified golden] ${goldenCase.caseId}`, () => {
   52:       const result = computeGst(makeRequest(goldenCase.input), resolution);
   53:       const expected = goldenCase.expected as Record<string, unknown>;
   54:       const output = result.output as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
   55:       for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(expected)) {
   56:         expect(
   57:           (output[key] as { minorUnits?: string })?.minorUnits ?? output[key],
   58:           key,
   59:         ).toEqual(value);
   60:       }
   61:     });
   62:   }
   63: 
   64:   it("sanity: fixture inputs parse and money uses minor-unit strings", () => {
   65:     expect(fixture.cases.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
   66:     expect(() => moneyFromMinorUnits("AUD", "10000", 2)).not.toThrow();
   67:   });
   68: });
### apps/web/lib/money-input.ts
    1: import { moneyFromDecimalString, type Money } from "@paymentcalcs/calculation-core";
    2: 
    3: export type MoneyParse =
    4:   | { ok: true; money: Money }
    5:   | { ok: false; error: string }
    6:   | { ok: false; error: null; empty: true };
    7: 
    8: /**
    9:  * Â§9.3 input behaviour: preserve partial entry, never coerce blank to zero,
   10:  * reject impossible values with remediation. Accepts "1,234.56", "1234.5",
   11:  * "-20". Rejects more than two decimal places rather than silently rounding.
   12:  */
   13: export function parseMoneyInput(raw: string, currency = "AUD"): MoneyParse {
   14:   const trimmed = raw.trim().replace(/,/g, "").replace(/^\$/, "");
   15:   if (trimmed === "" || trimmed === "-") return { ok: false, error: null, empty: true };
   16:   if (!/^-?\d+(\.\d{0,2})?$/.test(trimmed)) {
   17:     if (/^-?\d+\.\d{3,}$/.test(trimmed)) {
   18:       return { ok: false, error: "Use at most two decimal places (cents)." };
   19:     }
   20:     return { ok: false, error: "Enter an amount like 1,250.00." };
   21:   }
   22:   const normalised = trimmed.endsWith(".") ? trimmed.slice(0, -1) : trimmed;
   23:   return { ok: true, money: moneyFromDecimalString(currency, normalised, 2) };
   24: }

### packages/rule-schema/src/resolver.test.ts
    1: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
    2: import { computePackHash, manifestKey } from "./integrity";
    3: import { resolveRulePack } from "./resolver";
    4: import type { RulePackV1 } from "./schema";
    5: 
    6: function makePack(overrides: Partial<RulePackV1>): RulePackV1 {
    7:   return {
    8:     rulePackId: "test-pack",
    9:     jurisdiction: "AU",
   10:     subdivision: null,
   11:     domain: "test",
   12:     effectiveFrom: "2025-07-01",
   13:     effectiveTo: null,
   14:     status: "active",
   15:     schemaVersion: 1,
   16:     rulesVersion: "1.0.0",
   17:     sources: [
   18:       {
   19:         sourceId: "s1",
   20:         authority: "Test Authority",
   21:         title: "Test",
   22:         url: "https://example.gov.au/x",
   23:         jurisdiction: "AU",
   24:         domain: "test",
   25:         retrievedAt: "2026-08-20T00:00:00+08:00",
   26:       },
   27:     ],
   28:     review: { preparedBy: "p", approvedBy: "a", approvedAt: "2026-08-20T00:00:00+08:00" },
   29:     verifiedAt: "2026-08-20T00:00:00+08:00",
   30:     rules: { value: "1" },
   31:     ...overrides,
   32:   };
   33: }
   34: 
   35: async function manifestFor(...packs: RulePackV1[]) {
   36:   const manifest: Record<string, string> = {};
   37:   for (const pack of packs) manifest[manifestKey(pack)] = await computePackHash(pack);
   38:   return manifest;
   39: }
   40: 
   41: const query = { domain: "test", jurisdiction: "AU", valuationDate: "2026-08-20" } as const;
   42: 
   43: describe("resolver selection", () => {
   44:   it("prefers an active pack over a draft-runnable one even with allowDraftRules", async () => {
   45:     const active = makePack({ rulePackId: "older-active", effectiveFrom: "2024-07-01" });
   46:     const draft = makePack({
   47:       rulePackId: "newer-draft",
   48:       status: "in_review",
   49:       effectiveFrom: "2026-07-01",
   50:     });
   51:     const outcome = await resolveRulePack([draft, active], await manifestFor(active, draft), {
   52:       ...query,
   53:       allowDraftRules: true,
   54:     });
   55:     expect(outcome.ok && outcome.pack.rulePackId).toBe("older-active");
   56:     expect(outcome.ok && outcome.draft).toBe(false);
   57:   });
   58: 
   59:   it("picks the most recently effective among multiple active packs", async () => {
   60:     const older = makePack({ rulePackId: "fy-2025", effectiveFrom: "2025-07-01", effectiveTo: null });
   61:     const newer = makePack({ rulePackId: "fy-2026", effectiveFrom: "2026-07-01", effectiveTo: null });
   62:     const outcome = await resolveRulePack([older, newer], await manifestFor(older, newer), query);
   63:     expect(outcome.ok && outcome.pack.rulePackId).toBe("fy-2026");
   64:   });
   65: 
   66:   it("matches subdivision exactly when queried", async () => {
   67:     const nsw = makePack({ rulePackId: "nsw", subdivision: "NSW" });
   68:     const vic = makePack({ rulePackId: "vic", subdivision: "VIC" });
   69:     const outcome = await resolveRulePack([nsw, vic], await manifestFor(nsw, vic), {
   70:       ...query,
   71:       subdivision: "VIC",
   72:     });
   73:     expect(outcome.ok && outcome.pack.rulePackId).toBe("vic");
   74:   });
   75: 
   76:   it("respects effectiveTo â€” an expired pack does not cover a later date", async () => {
   77:     const expired = makePack({ effectiveTo: "2026-06-30" });
   78:     const outcome = await resolveRulePack([expired], await manifestFor(expired), query);
   79:     expect(outcome).toMatchObject({ ok: false, code: "PC-RULE-0001" });
   80:   });
   81: 
   82:   it("never runs withdrawn or superseded packs, even with allowDraftRules", async () => {
   83:     for (const status of ["withdrawn", "superseded", "corrected"] as const) {
   84:       const pack = makePack({ status });
   85:       const outcome = await resolveRulePack([pack], await manifestFor(pack), {
   86:         ...query,
   87:         allowDraftRules: true,
   88:       });
   89:       expect(outcome.ok, status).toBe(false);
   90:     }
   91:   });
   92: });
### packages/analytics-safe/src/redaction.test.ts
    1: import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
    2: import { createAnalytics, type AnalyticsProvider } from "./analytics";
    3: import { redactEvent } from "./redaction";
    4: 
    5: function expectRejected(event: string, props: Record<string, unknown>, key?: string) {
    6:   const outcome = redactEvent(event, props);
    7:   expect(outcome.accepted, JSON.stringify(props)).toBe(false);
    8:   if (!outcome.accepted && key !== undefined) {
    9:     expect(outcome.rejection.propertyKey).toBe(key);
   10:   }
   11: }
   12: 
   13: describe("Â§18.4 rejection â€” forbidden property classes never pass", () => {
   14:   it("rejects money amounts, however they arrive", () => {
   15:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { amount: 1234.56 }, "amount");
   16:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { salary: "120000" }, "salary");
   17:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { balance: "1,234.56" }, "balance");
   18:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { property_price: 950000 }, "property_price");
   19:   });
   20: 
   21:   it("rejects income, debt, tax settings, age and dates of birth", () => {
   22:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { income: 90000 }, "income");
   23:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { debt: "40000" }, "debt");
   24:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { tax_free_threshold: true }, "tax_free_threshold");
   25:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { age: 34 }, "age");
   26:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { dob: "1992-01-01" }, "dob");
   27:   });
   28: 
   29:   it("rejects rates as personal facts and free text", () => {
   30:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { interest_rate: 0.0619 }, "interest_rate");
   31:     expectRejected("scenario_action", { label: "our mortgage" }, "label");
   32:     expectRejected("scenario_action", { employer: "Acme Pty Ltd" }, "employer");
   33:     expectRejected("scenario_action", { note: "any free text" }, "note");
   34:   });
   35: 
   36:   it("rejects numbers wholesale, even under allowlisted keys", () => {
   37:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { calculator_id: 42 }, "calculator_id");
   38:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { has_warnings: 1 }, "has_warnings");
   39:   });
   40: 
   41:   it("rejects non-allowlisted events entirely", () => {
   42:     expectRejected("user_typed_value", {});
   43:   });
   44: 
   45:   it("rejects allowlisted keys whose values fail their validator", () => {
   46:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { calculator_id: "salary is 90k" }, "calculator_id");
   47:     expectRejected("calculation_completed", { financial_year: "ninety" }, "financial_year");
   48:   });
   49: 
   50:   it("drops the whole event when any property is rejected â€” no partial sends", () => {
   51:     const sent: unknown[] = [];
   52:     const provider: AnalyticsProvider = { send: (e, p) => sent.push([e, p]) };
   53:     const analytics = createAnalytics(provider);
   54:     analytics.track("calculation_completed", {
   55:       calculator_id: "AU-BIZ-001",
   56:       amount: 1234.56,
   57:     });
   58:     expect(sent).toHaveLength(0);
   59:   });
   60: });
   61: 
   62: describe("Â§18.4 allowed categorical metadata passes", () => {
   63:   it("accepts the PRD's example event", () => {
   64:     const outcome = redactEvent("calculation_completed", {
   65:       calculator_id: "AU-PAY-001",
   66:       mode: "advanced",
   67:       financial_year: "2026-27",
   68:       has_warnings: true,
   69:       duration_bucket: "under_100ms",
   70:     });
   71:     expect(outcome.accepted).toBe(true);
   72:   });
   73:

   75:     const sent: Array<[string, unknown]> = [];

   79:       rule_status: "unavailable",

### packages/eslint-config/src/rules/no-banned-result-verbs.mjs
    1: /**
    2:  * Non-negotiable #11: result-surface copy must never contain "should",
    3:  * "we recommend" or "best for you" (Â§17.9). Applied to result components and
    4:  * disclosure copy in calculation-ui and apps/web.
    5:  */
    6: const BANNED = /\b(should|we recommend|best for you)\b/i;
    7: 
    8: function check(context, node, text) {
    9:   const match = BANNED.exec(text);
   10:   if (match) {
   11:     context.report({
   12:       node,
   13:       messageId: "banned",
   14:       data: { phrase: match[0] },
   15:     });
   16:   }
   17: }
   18: 
   19: export default {
   20:   meta: {
   21:     type: "problem",
   22:     docs: {
   23:       description:
   24:         "disallow advice-implying verbs in result-surface copy (PRD Â§17.9, Â§20.10)",
   25:     },
   26:     messages: {
   27:       banned:
   28:         'Result copy must not contain "{{phrase}}" â€” rephrase without implying advice (PRD Â§17.9).',
   29:     },
   30:     schema: [],
   31:   },
   32:   create(context) {
   33:     return {
   34:       Literal(node) {
   35:         if (typeof node.value === "string") check(context, node, node.value);
   36:       },
   37:       TemplateElement(node) {
   38:         check(context, node, node.value.raw);
   39:       },
   40:       JSXText(node) {
   41:         check(context, node, node.value);
   42:       },
   43:     };
   44:   },
   45: };

docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-12-
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-13-## 2. Non-negotiables (release-gating, no exceptions)
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-14-
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-15-1. **Never write a tax rate, threshold, duty bracket, cap or levy from memory.** Your training data about Australian rates is presumed stale. Every jurisdiction- or year-dependent number lives in a rule pack (`packages/rules-au/`) with source URL, `retrievedAt`, and status — never in engine or UI code. If you can fetch the official source (ATO / state revenue office URLs in PRD §37.4–37.5), author a **draft** pack from it; if you cannot fetch, author the pack **structure** with `null` values. Either way, status is `in_review`, never `active`. Activation is a human action.
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md:16:2. **Engines are pure and deterministic.** `(request, rulePacks) => result` per the §14 contracts. No network, no `Date.now()`, no UI imports, no floating-point currency. Money is integer minor units (serialised as strings) per §14.1; rate/compounding maths uses `decimal.js`. Ban `number` for currency in engine packages via a lint rule and branded types.
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-17-3. **E02 (annual tax liability) and E03 (PAYG withholding) are separate engines with separately labelled outputs.** Never derive withholding as annual ÷ periods where an official schedule applies (§13.16, Merge Record #2). E03 consumes the official schedule formulas from rule packs.
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-18-4. **Mortgage scope = scheduled ledger** (Merge Record #3): payment-period ledger with dated/recurring events, offsets, IO periods, fees, reconciliation invariants (§12.5.8). Architecture must be daily-capable (day-count fields exist in contracts) but daily accrual mode is NOT built at P0. The Simulator route badges "Scheduled model".
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md:19:5. **Reconciliation is mandatory** for every schedule-producing engine (§13.30). A failed reconciliation is an engine failure state, never a cosmetic warning.
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-20-6. **Solvers** use monotonic bisection with the §13.28 safeguards. A solver must never return a plausible-looking number after failing to converge.
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-21-7. **No accounts, no database, no auth, no server-side scenario storage at P0.** Anonymous save = IndexedDB. Share = versioned URL-encoded state (Merge Record #5). All calculation runs client-side; heavy schedules run in a Web Worker (§22.4).
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-22-8. **Analytics redaction:** all analytics flow through `packages/analytics-safe`, which rejects any event property that is a money amount, income, balance, rate-as-personal-fact, age, or free text (§18.4). Write tests that prove rejection. Provider: Plausible script tag; no cookies, no consent banner.
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-23-9. **No LLM anywhere in the calculation path.** Deterministic engines only (PRD Fixed Decision #4).

docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-57-
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md:58:**Phase 0 — Foundation (Gate 0).** Read §11.2, §11.7, §14, §16, §20, §22, §27. Scaffold monorepo + CI (typecheck, lint incl. currency-number ban and banned-verbs check, Vitest, Playwright+axe, Lighthouse budget assertions). Build: design tokens (palette + type below), rule-schema + pack loader with integrity hash + status resolver + fail-closed behaviour, calculation request/result/scenario contracts (§14) with Zod, calculator-registry (machine-readable, §10.1 fields), analytics-safe wrapper + tests, calculator shell (§9.1 layouts, Simple/Advanced modes, explainability tabs §9.5, URL-state protocol, IndexedDB save, print CSS), the §20.12 standard states. Prove the template with **AU-BIZ-001 GST** end-to-end (Class A, minimal rule surface). Write `CLAUDE.md` (condense §2 non-negotiables + conventions) and `PROGRESS.md`. **Checkpoint: owner reviews shell + GST route before Phase 1.**
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-59-
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-60-**Phase 1 — Pay slice (Gate 1).** Read §12.1–12.2, §13.13–13.17, §13.27–13.28. Build E01 primitives, E02, E03, E04, E24 (bisection core). Author draft rule packs for FY2024-25, 2025-26, 2026-27 (income tax, offsets, Medicare/MLS, HELP marginal bands, SG + contribution base) from §37.4 sources per the §3 protocol. Ship the nine PAY routes with the §12.1 modes, output separation (liability vs withholding, PAY-AC-002), boundary tests at every bracket edge (PAY-AC-003), fixture scaffolds + verification queue. **Checkpoint: owner verifies rule packs + fixtures against ATO tools; external tax review booked.**
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-61-
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md:62:**Phase 2 — Mortgage slice (Gate 2).** Read §12.4–12.7, §11.5, §13.5–13.10, §13.30. Build E07 scheduled ledger (event timeline per §12.5.3 subset: rate change, recurring/one-off extra, offset events, fees, IO expiry; repayment reset policies; invariants §12.5.8; reconciliation), E11, Compare mode, timeline editor component, Web Worker execution for schedules. Ship the six HOME loan routes. Differential-test ledger vs closed-form (MORT-AC-001). **Checkpoint: owner review; lending reviewer engaged.**
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-63-

Build E08 + eight draft duty packs (each pack cites its revenue-office source; unsupported states blocked visibly), E10-lite (editable expense floors labelled editable defaults — no HEM claims), E12, E14, E19/E20 remainder. Ship remaining routes. Then the launch surface: category + all-calculators pages, `/methodology/` pages per route (formula registry references, §13.29), `/sources/`, `/changelog/`, `llms.txt`, robots + AI-crawler allows, per-route metadata/OG/schema (§24.4), noindex rules (§24.9), citable-atom worked examples rendered from fixtures, internal links per §24.8. Full a11y and performance audit against §27 budgets. **Checkpoint: launch-readiness report.**
docs/CLAUDE_CODE_P0_BUILD_PROMPT.md-65-

Do not deploy to production yourself.

    2:  * Deterministic serialisation and hashing for Â§14.3 integrity fields.
    3:  * Key order is normalised recursively so semantically identical documents
    4:  * always produce the same hash. Uses Web Crypto (Node â‰¥20 and all supported

    7: export function canonicalStringify(value: unknown): string {
    8:   return JSON.stringify(sortValue(value));
    9: }
   10: 
   11: function sortValue(value: unknown): unknown {
   12:   if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(sortValue);
   13:   if (value !== null && typeof value === "object") {
   14:     const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
   15:       .filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined)
   16:       .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));
   17:     return Object.fromEntries(entries.map(([k, v]) => [k, sortValue(v)]));
   18:   }
   19:   if (typeof value === "number" && !Number.isFinite(value)) {
   20:     throw new TypeError("non-finite number is not canonically serialisable");
   21:   }
   22:   return value;
   23: }
   24: 
   25: export async function sha256Hex(text: string): Promise<string> {
   26:   const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text);
   27:   const digest = await globalThis.crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", bytes);
   28:   return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest))
   29:     .map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
   30:     .join("");
   31: }

    3: import { useId, type ReactNode } from "react";

    5: /**
    6:  * Accessible segmented control (radiogroup). Used for Simple/Advanced modes
    7:  * and calculation-mode switches. Keyboard: arrow keys move selection.
    8:  */
    9: export function SegmentedControl<T extends string>({
   10:   label,
   11:   options,
   12:   value,
   13:   onChange,
   14:   size = "md",
   15: }: {
   16:   label: string;
   17:   options: ReadonlyArray<{ value: T; label: string }>;
   18:   value: T;
   19:   onChange: (value: T) => void;
   20:   size?: "sm" | "md";
   21: }) {
   22:   const groupId = useId();
   23:   const index = options.findIndex((o) => o.value === value);
   24: 
   25:   function onKeyDown(event: React.KeyboardEvent) {
   26:     const delta =
   27:       event.key === "ArrowRight" || event.key === "ArrowDown"
   28:         ? 1
   29:         : event.key === "ArrowLeft" || event.key === "ArrowUp"
   30:           ? -1
   31:           : 0;
   32:     if (delta === 0) return;
   33:     event.preventDefault();
   34:     const next = options[(index + delta + options.length) % options.length];
   35:     if (next) onChange(next.value);
   36:   }
   37: 
   38:   return (
   39:     <div
   40:       role="radiogroup"
   41:       aria-label={label}
   42:       onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
   43:       className="inline-flex max-w-full flex-wrap border border-hairline bg-surface"
   44:     >
   45:       {options.map((option) => {
   46:         const selected = option.value === value;
   47:         return (
   48:           <button
   49:             key={option.value}
   50:             role="radio"
   51:             aria-checked={selected}
   52:             tabIndex={selected ? 0 : -1}
   53:             id={`${groupId}-${option.value}`}
   54:             onClick={() => onChange(option.value)}
   55:             className={`min-h-9 border-r border-hairline px-4 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] transition-colors duration-[var(--pc-duration-fast)] ease-[var(--pc-ease)] last:border-r-0 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:outline-focus ${
   56:               selected
   57:                 ? "bg-accent text-accent-contrast"
   58:                 : "text-ink-2 hover:bg-surface-2 hover:text-ink"
   59:             } ${size === "sm" ? "min-h-8 px-3 text-[10px]" : ""}`}
   60:           >
   61:             {option.label}
   62:           </button>
   63:         );
   64:       })}
   65:     </div>

  138:                 <li key={source.sourceId} className="grid gap-1 py-3 first:pt-0 last:pb-0">
  139:                   <span className="text-[14px] text-ink">{source.title}</span>
  140:                   <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-2">{source.authority}</span>
  141:                   <a
  142:                     href={source.url}
  143:                     rel="noopener noreferrer"
  144:                     target="_blank"
  145:                     className="break-all font-mono text-[12px] text-info underline decoration-hairline-strong underline-offset-2 hover:decoration-current"
  146:                   >
  147:                     {source.url}
  148:                   </a>
  149:                   <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-3">
  150:                     Retrieved {source.retrievedAt.slice(0, 10)}
  151:                     {source.rulePackId ? ` Â· rule pack ${source.rulePackId}` : ""}
  152:                   </span>
  153:                 </li>
  154:               ))}
  155:             </ul>
  156:           ) : (
  157:             <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-3">No external sources apply to this calculation.</p>
  158:           )
  159:         ) : null}
  160: 
  161:         {active === "Limitations" ? (
  162:           <ul className="flex list-none flex-col gap-2">
  163:             {limitations.map((limitation) => (
  164:               <li key={limitation} className="border-l border-hairline-strong pl-4 text-[13px] leading-5 text-ink-2">
  165:                 {limitation}
  166:               </li>
  167:             ))}
  168:           </ul>
  169:         ) : null}
  170:       </div>
  171:     </section>
  172:   );
  173: }

codex
1. P0 — apps/web/lib/draft-rules.ts:11 — Production-mode preview builds can enable `in_review` rules, violating the requirement that draft rules be impossible in production. Fix: require `NODE_ENV === "development"` and fail production builds when the flag is set.

2. P0 — apps/web/app/layout.tsx:94 — Plausible’s automatic pageview bypasses `analytics-safe` and transmits the current URL, whose `s` parameter contains reversible financial inputs and free-text labels. Fix: disable automatic pageviews and emit sanitized path-only events through `analytics-safe`.

3. P1 — packages/engine-business/src/gst/engine.ts:280 — Invoice-total adjustment always recomputes `inclusiveAmount`, so an inclusive-priced line’s entered gross amount changes; two taxable 5-cent inclusive lines become an 11-cent invoice. Fix: preserve the entered basis, recomputing inclusive for exclusive lines and exclusive for inclusive lines.

4. P1 — packages/engine-business/src/gst/engine.ts:271 — The entire invoice rounding residual is assigned to the final taxable line, allowing many sub-cent residuals to create implausibly large or negative values on a small final line. Fix: distribute minor-unit adjustments across eligible lines by signed fractional remainder while preserving each line’s entered basis.

5. P1 — apps/web/components/gst/gst-calculator.tsx:160 — `Number.parseInt` silently converts quantities such as `1.9` or `1abc` to `1`, producing understated invoice totals without validation errors. Fix: validate the complete string as an integer and enforce the engine’s 1–9999 range.

6. P1 — packages/scenario-schema/src/url-state.ts:37 — Decoding validates only `calculatorId`, so payloads such as `{calculatorId:"AU-BIZ-001",input:null}` are accepted and crash the GST component when it dereferences `input`. Fix: parse a strict top-level schema and then a calculator-specific input-state schema before updating React state.

7. P1 — apps/web/components/gst/gst-calculator.tsx:127 — Shared scenarios silently truncate invoice state to 50 lines although the engine accepts 200, changing the recipient’s totals. Fix: use one shared line limit across editor, URL schema, and engine, or reject oversized shares explicitly.

8. P1 — apps/web/components/gst/gst-calculator.tsx:68 — `toISOString()` derives the valuation date in UTC, selecting the previous day for users in Perth before 08:00 and potentially resolving an expired rule pack at effective-date boundaries. Fix: derive the date in the calculator jurisdiction’s timezone.

9. P1 — apps/web/components/gst/gst-calculator.tsx:397 — Failed and invalid engine results have no output and are rendered as the generic “Enter an amount” state, while line 217 records them as `calculation_completed`. Fix: branch on `result.status`, render the engine errors, and emit `calculation_failed`.

10. P1 — apps/web/lib/scenario-store.ts:33 — IndexedDB operations resolve on request success before transaction commit, so a later abort can still display “Saved”; abort/error paths also leave the database connection open. Fix: retain the request result, resolve on `transaction.oncomplete`, reject on `onerror`/`onabort`, and close on every terminal path.

11. P1 — packages/calculator-registry/src/entries.ts:24 — The production SEO title hard-codes the statutory `10%` rate outside `packages/rules-au`. Fix: use rate-neutral metadata or generate rate-bearing metadata exclusively from a successfully resolved active pack.

12. CLEAN — packages/rules-au/src/packs/au-gst-standard.json:8 — The current GST pack is `in_review`, sourced, unverified, and integrity-pinned.

13. CLEAN — packages/engine-business/src/gst/engine.ts:324 — Engine arithmetic uses Decimal/Money primitives, has no clock/network/UI dependency, and returns `failed` when reconciliation fails.

14. CLEAN — packages/scenario-schema/src/url-state.ts:30 — No executable XSS or prototype-pollution path was found in current consumers; React escapes decoded labels and object spread does not invoke the legacy `__proto__` setter.

15. CLEAN — packages/eslint-config/src/rules/no-banned-result-verbs.mjs:6 — No runtime result copy contains “should”, “we recommend”, or “best for you”.
